Conclusion: 对角线性网络在无穷小初始化下的隐式偏好可由修正ℓ1范数刻画，结构不变流形是理解其学习动力学的核心几何结构。

Abstract: We study the gradient flow dynamics of diagonal linear networks for regression tasks under infinitesimal initialization. Extending Theorem 1 from Pesme & Flammarion (2023), we generalize the analysis to both deep diagonal linear networks and a broader class of two-layer diagonal linear networks (as defined in Definition 4.1). Specifically, we demonstrate that the training trajectories of these models can be equivalently characterized by the proposed Algorithm 1. We further prove that this algorithm converges to the solution of a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm minimization problem. As a result, we establish that the implicit bias of both network architectures corresponds to a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm in the regime of infinitesimal initialization. Additionally, we provide insights into the underlying mechanisms governing these dynamics by identifying the Structural Invariant Manifold (SIM) (Zhao et al., 2026) as the key geometric structure that shapes the learning process.

</details>


### [92] [Generating Developable 3D Molecules via Pocket-Conditioned Diffusion and Property-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12349)
*Ruoxi Gao,Jiangweizhi Peng,Ziqi Chen,Frazier N. Baker,David C. Kombo,John L. Kane,Andrew A. Scholte,Yi Li,Matthew J. LaMarche,Luigi I. Iconaru,Hans-Peter Biemann,Mingyi Hong,Xia Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了conDitar-dev，一个基于条件扩散模型的结构化药物设计框架，通过多尺度口袋表征学习、条件扩散生成和生成时优化三个模块，在人类疾病靶标上实现了优异的结合亲和力（平均-8.85 kcal/mol）和ADMET性质（相对提升高达73%），并在PD-L1和CSF1R靶点上通过实验验证，获得了μM级别的SPR亲和力和nM级别的IC50活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的结构化药物设计方法存在三个主要问题：口袋和配体表征学习耦合、仅在原子层面建模相互作用、优先考虑结合亲和力而忽视其他药物开发性质（如ADMET）。因此需要一种能同时优化结合亲和力和药物可开发性的生成框架。

Method: 提出三模块框架：(1) msPRL：预训练的多尺度口袋表征学习模块；(2) conDitar：由msPRL表征引导的口袋条件扩散模型；(3) paOPT：生成时优化配体药物可开发性的方法。在人类疾病靶标的新基准上进行评估，并在PD-L1和CSF1R靶点上进行实验验证。

Result: conDitar在人类疾病靶标上平均结合分数为-8.85 kcal/mol，优于现有SBDD基线。conDitar-dev在五个ADMET性质上相较conDitar提升高达73%。针对PD-L1生成的分子的SPR KD值分别为3.49和3.75 μM；针对CSF1R的分子经hit expansion获得IC50低至200 nM的选择性抑制剂。

Conclusion: conDitar-dev能够生成兼具强结合亲和力和良好ADMET性质的可开发分子，实验结果验证了其在实际药物发现中的有效性，并发现了药物重定位的机会。

Abstract: Drug discovery and development is time-consuming and resource-intensive, motivating computational approaches such as diffusion models for de novo drug design. Many such models follow the structure-based drug design (SBDD) paradigm, generating molecules to fit a target binding pocket. However, existing diffusion-based SBDD methods typically couple pocket and ligand representation learning, model interactions only at the atom level, and prioritize binding affinity over other developability properties. Here, we introduce conDitar-dev, a conditional diffusion-based SBDD framework for generating ligands with strong binding affinities and favorable ADMET properties. It consists of three modules: msPRL, a pretrained multi-scale pocket representation learning module; conDitar, a pocket-conditioned diffusion model guided by msPRL representations; and paOPT, a generation-time method for optimizing ligand developability. On a newly curated benchmark of human disease targets, conDitar outperforms state-of-the-art SBDD baselines, achieving an average binding score of -8.85 kcal/mol. Across five ADMET properties, conDitar-dev improves performance by up to 73% over conDitar. To further validate the abilities of conDitar-dev to generate developable molecules, we have applied it to two validated druggable targets: programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) and colony-stimulating factor 1 receptor (CSF1R) proteins. Top-ranked generatively designed molecules and their analogs have been experimentally synthesized and biologically tested. Two molecules generated directly by conDitar-dev for PD-L1 exhibited SPR-derived $K_D$ values of 3.49 and 3.75 $μ$M, respectively. Hit expansion based on conDitar-dev-designed molecules identified selective CSF1R inhibitors with IC$_{50}$ values as low as 200 nM, while also uncovering opportunities for drug repositioning.

</details>


### [93] [Reducing information dependency does not cause training data privacy. Adversarially non-robust features do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12354)
*Rasmus Torp,Shailen K. Smith,Adam Breuer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“信息依赖（包括死记硬背）导致训练数据暴露于图像重构攻击”的主流观点。作者发现，即使没有死记硬背，模型仍可被严重重构，其根本原因在于与对抗鲁棒性相关的“非鲁棒特征”。他们提出了一种反对抗训练（AT-AT）方法，通过有意学习非鲁棒特征，在防御重构攻击和准确性上均超越现有方法，揭示了一个新的隐私-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前主流观点，即认为训练数据暴露于图像重构攻击（如模型反转攻击MIA）主要由信息依赖（包括死记硬背）驱动。作者通过三个反直觉的实验结果发现现有理论无法解释的现象，进而探索真正的因果机制。

Method: 论文首先通过三个反直觉实验揭示传统信息依赖理论的不足。然后引入对抗样本文献中的“非鲁棒特征”概念，通过因果分析证明MIA下的隐私泄露实际上来源于这些可泛化但不可感知、不稳定的特征。最后提出反对抗训练（AT-AT），有意学习非鲁棒特征以同时实现优异的重构防御和高准确率。

Result: (1) 抑制MIA重构的防御并未降低HSIC等标准信息依赖指标；(2) 最大程度记忆训练数据的模型对MIA重构仍具有鲁棒性；(3) 未见97%训练像素的模型仍可被MIA严重重构。(4) AT-AT方法在防御重构和准确率上均超越现有最先进防御。

Conclusion: 训练数据暴露并非由死记硬背或信息依赖驱动，而是由模型学习到的“非鲁棒特征”导致。MIA防御通过无意中使模型偏向这些特征来获得隐私提升。这修正了对训练数据暴露的主流理解，并揭示了一个新的隐私-鲁棒性权衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we challenge the prevailing view that information dependency (including rote memorization) drives training data exposure to image reconstruction attacks. We show that extensive exposure can persist without rote memorization and is instead caused by a tunable connection to adversarial robustness. We begin by presenting three surprising results: (1) recent defenses that inhibit reconstruction by Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs), which evaluate leakage under an idealized attacker, do not reduce standard measures of information dependency (HSIC); (2) models that maximally memorize their training datasets remain robust to MIA reconstruction; and (3) models trained without seeing 97% of the training pixels, where recent information-theoretic bounds give arbitrarily strong privacy guarantees under standard assumptions, can still be devastatingly reconstructed by MIA.
  To explain these findings, we provide causal evidence that privacy under MIA arises from what the adversarial examples literature calls ``non-robust'' features (generalizable but imperceptible and unstable features). We further show that recent MIA defenses obtain their privacy improvements by unintentionally shifting models toward such features. To establish this causal relationship, we introduce Anti Adversarial Training (AT-AT), a training regime that intentionally learns non-robust features to obtain both superior reconstruction defense and higher accuracy than state-of-the-art defenses. Our results revise the prevailing understanding of training data exposure and reveal a new privacy-robustness tradeoff.

</details>


### [94] [Same Loss, Same Noise, Opposite Schedules: Noise Structure and Optimizer Normalization Jointly Determine Whether Learning-Rate Cooldown Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12360)
*Subham Singh,Ashutosh Mishra,Subha Raut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了在WSD学习率调度中，cool-down阶段是否有效取决于两个因素：梯度噪声的结构（乘法噪声 vs 加法噪声）和优化器是否归一化更新步长。SGD在乘法噪声下无需cool-down即可收敛，而sign-based和归一化方法必须将学习率驱零才能达到最优解，这解释了不同场景下cool-down效果差异的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSD学习率调度中的cool-down阶段在大模型预训练中被广泛使用，但它在某些设置下能降低最终训练损失，在其他设置下却无效。作者希望从理论上解释这种差异的根本原因。

Method: 作者在强凸目标函数下，分别考虑乘法噪声（梯度比例噪声）和加法噪声，分析了SGD、signSGD和归一化方法的收敛行为。通过求解signSGD在二次型上的平稳分布（得到闭式解）、在(L0,L1)-光滑性下证明局部解耦形式、利用尺度不变性论证将结论推广到d>1维，并验证了对动量和重尾噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: SGD在乘法噪声下以常数学习率几何收缩，无需cool-down；sign-based和归一化方法在相同条件下会停留在η²量级的噪声底板上，只有将学习率驱零才能达到最优解。加法噪声会使所有方法都产生噪声底板。实验验证了所有预测，并在真实分类任务上通过直接测量梯度噪声展示了噪声机制诊断。

Conclusion: cool-down是否有效取决于梯度噪声结构（乘法 vs 加法）和优化器是否归一化步长。SGD步长与梯度成比例，具有自退火性质；归一化步长始终为单位尺度，无法自退火，因此必须依赖cool-down将学习率驱零。该机制还可解释大规模训练中内部cool-down比例的选择依据。

Abstract: The cooldown phase of a warmup-stable-decay (WSD) learning-rate schedule, now a default in large-model pretraining, lowers the final training loss in some settings and does nothing in others. We give a provable account of which case obtains, and it turns on two properties together: the structure of the gradient noise and whether the optimizer normalizes its update. On a strongly convex objective with multiplicative (gradient-proportional) noise, stochastic gradient descent contracts geometrically at a constant learning rate, so cooldown has nothing to improve. Under the same objective and noise, sign-based and normalized methods, the standard surrogates for adaptive optimizers, settle on a noise floor of order $η^2$ and reach the minimizer only as the learning rate is driven to zero; any additive noise then reinstates a floor for every method. The mechanism is elementary: an SGD step shrinks in proportion to the gradient and so anneals itself, whereas a normalized step keeps unit scale and cannot. We solve the signSGD stationary law on the quadratic exactly and obtain the floor constant in closed form, prove a local form of the dissociation under $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness, extend the floor to normalized SGD in dimension d>1 by a scale-invariance argument, and establish robustness to momentum and heavy-tailed noise. Simulation confirms every prediction, and we demonstrate the resulting noise-regime diagnostic on a real classification task with directly measured gradient noise. The mechanism explains whether cooldown helps; the interior cooldown fraction used at scale lies outside stationary landscape-and-noise geometry.

</details>


### [95] [SinAE: A Single-Architecture Flow-Matching Autoencoder for Cross-Domain Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12380)
*Yuxuan Ren,Fan Yang,Jianhua Yao,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinAE 是一种统一的流匹配自编码器架构，使用普通 Transformer 替代图/等变/框架结构，通过迭代流匹配解码器实现分子、晶体和蛋白质的近乎无损重建，并在跨域联合训练中验证了共享原子潜码的迁移优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子、晶体和蛋白质在3D空间中本质上都是原子的排列，但现有的生成管线因领域而异，分别使用图神经网络、等变网络或基于框架的架构。跨域训练可以缓解各领域数据稀缺问题，但直接在3D坐标空间生成难以处理三者异构的结构先验，且现有潜自编码器无法同时实现无损重建和架构通用性。

Method: SinAE 采用单一架构的流匹配自编码器：编码器和解码器均为普通 Transformer，不使用等变、图或领域特定算子。编码器不必捕捉精细几何信息，而是将重建负担转移给迭代式流匹配解码器，从而实现跨领域的近乎无损重建。潜空间采用标准的扩散先验（Diffusion Transformer）进行生成。

Result: 与现有潜基线方法相比，重建误差降低了数个数量级；在分子、晶体和蛋白质的生成基准上均达到强性能；分子-晶体联合训练严格提升了两个领域的表现，证实了通过共享原子潜码实现的跨域迁移。

Conclusion: SinAE 证明了一个简单的非等变 Transformer 架构，配合流匹配解码器，能够在分子、晶体和蛋白质三个领域实现统一的、近乎无损的潜表示学习和生成，且跨域联合训练能带来性能提升。

Abstract: Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its own graph, equivariant, or frame-based architecture. Cross-domain training would mitigate per-domain data scarcity, but direct generation in 3D coordinate space cannot easily handle the heterogeneous structural priors of all three domains, and no prior latent autoencoder is simultaneously lossless and architecturally general across all three. We introduce SinAE, a single-architecture flow-matching autoencoder for molecules, crystals, and proteins, with vanilla Transformer encoder and decoder and no equivariant, graph, or domain-specific operators. Rather than requiring the encoder to capture fine-grained geometry, SinAE shifts the reconstruction burden into an iterative flow-matching decoder, achieving near-lossless reconstruction across domains and reducing reconstruction errors by orders of magnitude relative to prior latent baselines. The same per-token latent supports a standard Diffusion Transformer prior that reaches strong performance on molecular, crystal, and protein generation benchmarks. Joint molecule--crystal training strictly improves both domains, providing direct evidence of cross-domain transfer through a shared atomic latent. Code is available at https://github.com/BlueWhaleLab/SinAE .

</details>


### [96] [Differentiable Clone-Structured Causal Graphs for End-to-End Cognitive Map Learning from Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12382)
*Arash Nikzad,Sasan Sarbishegi,Ali Dasmeh,Muhammad Asif,Parsa Gharavi,Erik Husom,Sagar Sen,Andrew B. Lehr,Olivier Penacchio,Ana Clemente,Tristan M. Stöber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出gradCSCG，将Clone-Structured Causal Graph (CSCG) 算法重新表述为完全可微模块，并与VQ-VAE感知前端结合，实现从原始图像序列端到端学习结构化地图，在符号网格世界和MNIST图像序列中均能鲁棒恢复底层邻接图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSCG作为规范的海马体模型能从别名观测中学习可解释地图，但需要预定义离散字母表且其期望最大化公式难以与神经网络模块结合，无法实现原始图像序列的端到端处理。作者旨在移除这一障碍。

Method: 将CSCG重新表述为完全可微模块gradCSCG，耦合VQ-VAE感知前端；设计软发射前向传播使地图学习目标反向传播到感知模块；引入损失平衡机制缓解联合训练中的模块崩溃。

Result: 梯度训练在符号网格世界再现CSCG结果，恢复房间拓扑；在MNIST图像序列上地图恢复保持鲁棒；在四个高别名环境中，端到端流水线从视觉输入直接成功发现底层邻接图，具有高边精度和召回率。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了CSCG可作为深度学习架构中可组合构建块的原理证明。

Abstract: How can an agent build a structured map of its world from nothing but an ongoing sequence of raw sensory input and its own movements, especially when natural variation means exact sensory patterns rarely repeat? The Clone-Structured Causal Graph algorithm (CSCG), a normative hippocampus model, shows how an interpretable map can be learned from aliased observations. However, CSCG requires a predefined discrete alphabet, and its expectation-maximization formulation is not easily combined with existing neural network modules, preventing the end-to-end processing of raw image sequences. We remove this barrier by reformulating CSCG as a single, fully differentiable module, gradCSCG, and coupling it to a learned vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) perceptual front-end. A soft emission forward pass allows the map-learning objective to flow back into perception, while a set of loss-balancing mechanisms mitigates module collapse during joint training. We demonstrate, first, that gradient training reproduces CSCG's results on original symbolic grid worlds by recovering room topology from heavily aliased observations. Second, we show that map recovery remains robust on MNIST image sequences, where each visit to a location yields a newly sampled image of its assigned digit. Across four heavily aliased environments, the end-to-end pipeline successfully uncovers the underlying adjacency graph with high edge precision and recall, directly from visual input. This work provides a proof of principle that CSCG can serve as a composable building block in a deep learning architecture.

</details>


### [97] [ReDiTT: Retrieval Augmented Conditional Diffusion Transformers for Asynchronous Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12391)
*Saiyue Lyu,Zhitian Zhang,Ruizhi Deng,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的异步时间序列预测方法ReDiTT，通过检索增强的条件扩散Transformer在潜空间中进行操作，利用记忆库中结构相似的潜序列作为参考条件，在下一个事件预测和长时域预测上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步时间序列中未来事件存在固有不确定性，现有方法在长时域预测中稳定性不足且样本多样性有限。

Method: ReDiTT：检索增强的条件扩散Transformer，在潜空间运行；训练和推理时从记忆库检索结构相似的潜序列，通过交叉注意力将其作为参考条件注入模型。

Result: 在7个真实数据集上，下一个事件预测和长时域预测均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 检索增强的条件机制能够稳定长时域预测并提高样本多样性，验证了检索式条件在异步时间序列扩散模型中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a diffusion based model for asynchronous time series prediction, where the goal is to predict the next inter event time and event type. To address the inherent uncertainty of future events, we introduce ReDiTT, a retrieval augmented conditional diffusion transformer that operates in latent space. ReDiTT retrieves structurally similar latent sequences from a memory bank during both training and inference and incorporates them as reference conditions through cross attention. This retrieval based conditioning allows the model to attend to relevant temporal dynamics and provides global structural guidance for generation. As a result, ReDiTT stabilizes long horizon forecasting and improves sample diversity. Experiments on seven real world datasets demonstrate state of the art performance on next event prediction and long horizon forecasting. Our code is available at https://github.com/BorealisAI/ReDiTT.

</details>


### [98] [Mechanical Analysis of Parachute Suspension Line Deployment with Binding Tapes Using PINN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12409)
*Xiang Zhao,Ronghui Quan,Yaqi Xiao,Junlin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络(PINN)的算法，用于预测降落伞伞绳抽绳拉直过程中的张力分布，相比传统数值积分方法在计算效率和精度上均有提升，并通过飞行试验数据验证了方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用常微分方程数值积分方法计算伞绳张力，但该方法无法快速获取伞绳任意位置处的张力值，需要开发更高效精确的张力预测算法。

Method: 开发了一种物理信息神经网络(PINN)算法，用于伞绳抽绳拉直过程中的张力预测，并研究了绑带参数对绳动态张力的调控规律。

Result: 该方法在计算效率和数值精度上均优于传统积分方法，并通过与飞行试验数据和传统数值结果的对比验证了其可靠性和有效性。

Conclusion: PINN框架为降落伞伞绳张力预测提供了一种高效精确的新方案，并揭示了绑带参数对张力的调控规律。

Abstract: Parachutes are widely utilized in aviation, aerospace and lifesaving missions. As the initial stage of parachute deployment, suspension line extraction and straightening directly determines the smooth implementation of subsequent inflation procedures. This ultra-short process involves intricate dynamic load variations. Most existing studies adopt numerical integration of ordinary differential equations to calculate line tension, yet this method fails to rapidly acquire tension values at arbitrary positions along suspension lines. This paper develops a physics-informed neural network (PINN) algorithm for tension prediction during line extraction and straightening, which outperforms traditional integration methods in both computational efficiency and numerical accuracy. Furthermore, the regulatory law of binding tape parameters on line dynamic tension is investigated. Comparative validations against flight test data and conventional numerical results verify the reliability and effectiveness of the proposed PINN framework.

</details>


### [99] [PolarBM: Complex-valued Boltzmann Machine for Modeling Audio Signals in Polar and Log-polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12417)
*Toru Nakashika,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolarBM和LogPolarBM，这两种玻尔兹曼机能够自然地处理极坐标下的复数值变量，显式建模振幅与相位之间的依赖关系。实验表明，在音频信号处理中，其受限变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM在建模精度上优于传统模型，包括深度神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法在处理复数数据（如音频频谱）时，常采用实数值框架进行简化，这虽然降低了计算复杂度，但丢弃了振幅与相位之间固有的结构性关系信息。本文旨在提出一种能够自然处理复数值变量并保留振幅-相位依赖关系的概率模型。

Method: 提出PolarBM，在极坐标系下为复变量定义概率密度函数，使相位显式依赖于振幅；进一步提出LogPolarBM，在对数尺度上建模振幅，导出了功率加权非中心复高斯（PW-NCCG）分布，其边缘振幅分布涵盖Rice、Nakagami和非中心卡方分布作为特例。同时提出对应的受限变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过显式建模振幅与相位的依赖关系，所提出的RBM在音频信号建模精度上优于传统模型及深度神经网络。

Conclusion: 所提出的玻尔兹曼机不仅适用于音频信号，其应用潜力还可扩展至无线通信、量子力学等涉及复数数据的科学与工程领域。

Abstract: Although vast amounts of data, such as audio signal spectra, are naturally represented using complex numbers, conventional machine learning methods often simplify complex-domain problems by employing frameworks designed for real-valued variables. While this simplification offers computational benefits, it discards structural information regarding the inherent relationship between amplitude and phase. In this paper, we propose a novel Boltzmann machine (BM), named PolarBM, capable of naturally handling complex-valued variables in the polar coordinate (i.e., an amplitude-phase representation). PolarBM defines a probability density function for complex variables in which the phase explicitly depends on the amplitude, thereby capturing the physically important relationships of complex-valued signals. Furthermore, to process audio signals in accordance with human auditory perception, we propose LogPolarBM, which models amplitude on a logarithmic scale. This extension yields a flexible conditional probability density function, a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian (PW-NCCG) distribution, whose marginal amplitude distribution encompasses the Rice, Nakagami, and noncentral chi distributions as special cases. For practical applications, we also introduce the restricted variants of these proposed models: PolarRBM and LogPolarRBM. Experimental results demonstrate that by explicitly modeling the dependency between amplitude and phase, the proposed RBMs achieve superior modeling accuracy compared to conventional models, including deep neural networks. Although our experiments focus on audio signals, the utility of the proposed BMs is not limited to audio applications; their potential extends widely across various fields of science and engineering that involve complex-valued data, such as wireless communications and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [100] [Fisher Rank Inflation: A Spectral Signature of Memorization under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12438)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度网络在记忆噪声标签时会经历一个谱特征变化：Fisher有效秩先膨胀后收缩，称为Fisher秩膨胀现象。该现象与记忆阶段对齐，可用于识别噪声样本、衡量噪声严重程度，并在测试性能下降前预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在噪声标签训练中会先学习干净结构再记忆噪声标签，但缺乏可观测的谱级信号来标记这一转变过程。本文旨在揭示该转变的谱特征，并利用其识别噪声样本和量化噪声严重程度。

Method: 通过对逐样本最后一层梯度的中心化散度矩阵进行谱分析，定义Fisher有效秩；推导一阶留一法属性归因公式；在CIFAR-10/100/10N上用SmallCNN、ResNet18和Vision Transformer进行实验验证。

Result: Fisher有效秩呈现一致的膨胀-收缩轨迹；峰值处噪声样本在最高贡献样本中占比69.2%-96.2%；有效秩随噪声率单调增加（干净训练28.88→60%噪声97.09）；秩膨胀早于测试性能下降。

Conclusion: Fisher秩膨胀是连接噪声样本富集、噪声严重程度以及从结构学习到记忆转变的谱特征信号，为理解标签噪声下的深度学习动态提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Deep networks trained with label noise often learn clean structure before memorizing corrupted labels. We show that this transition leaves a spectral signature in the centered scatter of per-example last-layer gradients. Its effective rank transiently expands during memorization and contracts after corrupted labels are fit. We call this phenomenon Fisher Rank Inflation. Corrupted labels increase effective rank by injecting spectral mass into low-energy or previously unused eigendirections, increasing the entropy of the gradient spectrum. We derive a first-order leave-one-out attribution formula, identify conditions under which corrupted examples contribute more strongly than clean examples, and explain why attribution signals weaken once the normalized Fisher-gradient spectrum stabilizes. We test these predictions on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CIFAR-10N using SmallCNN, ResNet18, and Vision Transformers. Across settings, Fisher effective rank exhibits a consistent inflation--collapse trajectory aligned with memorization. At peak-rank checkpoints, corrupted examples are enriched among the highest rank-contributing samples, with top-100 noisy fractions from \(69.2\%\) to \(96.2\%\) across five-seed synthetic-corruption experiments and \(94.4\%\pm1.9\%\) on CIFAR-10N. First-order spectral attribution closely matches exact leave-one-out contributions in convolutional models and remains enriched in the Vision Transformer. Peak effective rank increases monotonically with corruption severity, from \(28.88\pm1.95\) under clean training to \(97.09\pm1.78\) at \(60\%\) corruption. In several settings, the retrospectively identified onset of rank inflation precedes observable test degradation. These results establish Fisher Rank Inflation as a spectral signature connecting corrupted-example enrichment, corruption severity, and the transition from structure learning to memorization.

</details>


### [101] [The Computational Basis of Confidence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12447)
*Dharshan Kumaran,Viorica Patraucean,Maks Ovsanikov,Petar Veličković,Nathaniel Daw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统计决策置信度（SDC）框架，测试多模态语言模型中答案logit差（LD）是否反映潜在决策变量而非启发式偏好。在感知分辨等任务中，LD满足SDC定性特征（包括诊断性的正确/错误折叠X模式），表明logits作为决策变量单调读出；但在复杂视觉推理中完整几何特征消失，揭示了当前框架的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅通过预测正确性和校准度评估置信度，但未回答置信度信号本身代表什么——是反映潜在决策变量的贝叶斯置信度，还是非贝叶斯的启发式偏好信号。

Method: 采用计算神经科学中的统计决策置信度（SDC）框架，将答案logit差（LD）作为潜在决策变量的候选读出，测试SDC预测的定性特征（如折叠X模式）。在三个感知辨别任务、一个记忆决策任务及复杂视觉推理任务上进行验证，涵盖三个多模态非推理模型和一个推理模型。

Result: 在感知和记忆任务中，LD满足SDC的定性特征（包括诊断性的正确/错误折叠X模式），表明logits作为决策变量单调读出；在复杂视觉推理中，LD仍能预测正确性，但SDC完整几何特征消失。

Conclusion: 提供了多模态语言模型置信度的计算解释，划定了logits何时作为潜在决策变量读出的边界，并建立了SDC作为跨越生物和人工智能研究置信度的统一框架。

Abstract: Reliable confidence -- the probability that a model's own answer is correct -- is essential for the trustworthy deployment of language models. Existing work has largely evaluated confidence by how well it predicts correctness and whether it is calibrated, leaving open a more fundamental question: what does the confidence signal itself represent? Answer logits may reflect a latent decision variable sufficient to compute normative confidence, or instead a heuristic preference signal that combines the available evidence in a non-Bayesian manner. We address this using statistical decision confidence (SDC), a normative framework from computational neuroscience. Treating the answer-logit difference (LD) as a candidate readout of the latent decision variable, we test the qualitative signatures predicted by SDC. Across three perceptual discrimination tasks and a memory-based decision task, spanning three multimodal non-reasoning models and one reasoning model, LD satisfied these signatures -- including the diagnostic correct/error folded-X pattern -- showing that, in these settings, answer logits behave as monotonic readouts of a latent decision variable rather than heuristic preference scores. In complex visual reasoning, LD continued to predict correctness beyond objective task difficulty, but the full geometric signatures of SDC were absent, illustrating the current boundary of the framework when explicit normative process models are unavailable. These results provide a computational account of confidence in multimodal language models, delineate when answer logits behave as readouts of a latent decision variable, and establish SDC as a unifying framework for studying confidence across biological and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [102] [Exploring Zero-Shot Foundation Models for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12454)
*Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Roland Kwitt,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索将单变量时序基础模型TimesFM零样本应用于多变量时序异常检测（MTSAD），在SWaT基准上测试了两种策略。结果表明零样本FMs在MTSAD上无法与已有基线竞争，主要原因是模型过于擅长捕捉时序动态，导致异常窗口内预测误差过低而无法区分。但误差在异常边界处出现峰值，表明模型在变化点检测方面具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MTSAD方法依赖特定应用模型，训练成本高且难以扩展。近期出现的单变量时序基础模型具有强大的零样本泛化能力，作者希望探究这些模型是否能在无需任务特定训练的情况下解决MTSAD问题。

Method: 采用TimesFM作为单变量预测基础模型，在SWaT数据集上测试两种零样本策略：(1) 作为逐特征预测器，基于预测误差阈值判定异常；(2) 作为嵌入器，利用中间层表征输入标准异常检测器。

Result: 两种策略均无法与现有基线方法竞争。嵌入表征仅能部分区分正常与异常片段，不足以实现可靠检测。原因在于模型对时序动态的捕捉能力过强，即使在完全异常的窗口内也能保持低预测误差，使得持续异常与正常行为难以区分。但观察到预测误差在异常边界处出现峰值。

Conclusion: 所提出的朴素零样本FMs方案不适合多变量时序异常检测任务，但在变化点检测任务上具有前景。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is essential for reliability and safety in domains such as industrial process monitoring and financial risk management, yet conventional approaches rely on application-specific models that are costly to train and hard to scale. Foundation Models (FMs), pre-trained on broad data with strong zero-shot generalization, have recently become available for univariate time series forecasting, raising the question of whether they can address MTSAD without task-specific training. We investigate the zero-shot application of a univariate forecasting FM, TimesFM, to industrial MTSAD on the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark, evaluating two strategies: treating the FM as a per-feature forecaster with thresholded prediction errors, and as an embedder whose intermediate representations feed standard outlier detectors. Neither of our proposed setups is competitive with established baselines; embeddings reveal only partial separation between normal and anomalous segments, insufficient for reliable detection. The cause is that the FM is too effective at capturing temporal dynamics, yielding low error even within fully anomalous windows, so persistent anomalies become indistinguishable from normal behavior. However, these observations yield valuable insights: the error peaks at anomaly boundaries, indicating FMs reliably detect distribution changes. We conclude that the proposed naive zero-shot FMs are unsuitable for MTSAD but promising for change-point detection.

</details>


### [103] [Extractable Memorization From First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12649)
*A. Feder Cooper,Marika Swanberg,Jamie Hayes,Lea Duesterwald,Christopher De Sa,Daniel E. Ho,Mark A. Lemley,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM可提取记忆研究中两种相反的效度问题，提出了匹配比较方法（包括共形检验和普查），通过对比训练序列与非训练序列的生成概率来严格校准记忆化声明，并重新定义了"可提取记忆"的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆化提取研究存在两个相反的有效性问题：一些研究过度声称记忆化（依赖过短序列，无法区分记忆化与可预测性），另一些研究则暗示提取不可靠（模型也能生成未训练的真实文本）。两者都忽略了有效提取声明的关键：模型生成训练序列的概率必须足够高以表明记忆化。

Method: 提出两种匹配比较形式化方法：(1) 共形检验（conformal test）：在训练和非训练序列从总体中采样时，校准到选定假阳性率(FPR)的阈值；(2) 普查（census）：当对象为单个文档（如书籍）时，针对匹配的非训练文档进行校准。

Result: 在Wikipedia上，OLMo 2 32B生成非训练的10-token后缀的频率约为训练序列的24%，表明该比例中存在假阳性而非记忆化。对于Llama 3.1 70B在书籍上的实验，校准阈值低至1e-27，支持对任何可行采样预算都无法提取的序列的记忆化声明。

Conclusion: 匹配比较方法能够实现严格、校准的记忆化声明，并揭示了先前实验设置的效度问题。基于结果，将"可提取记忆"重新定义为：需要有效的记忆化声明，以及在现实预算内近乎确定的生成能力。

Abstract: Recent work on extractable memorization in LLMs suffers from two contrasting validity problems. Some studies overstate extraction, e.g., relying on sequences too short to distinguish memorization from predictability. Others imply that extraction is unreliable evidence of memorization, since models can also reproduce real-world text they weren't explicitly trained on. In different ways, both overlook what makes a valid extraction claim: the model must generate a training sequence with high enough probability to indicate memorization. To determine what's high enough, one has to perform a matched comparison: measuring the generation probabilities of both the training sequences of interest and comparable non-training sequences. Because non-training sequences cannot have been memorized, their probabilities provide a baseline for predictability; a training sequence exceeding this baseline provides evidence of memorization. We formalize matched comparisons in two ways: (1) a conformal test that calibrates a threshold to a chosen FPR when training and non-training sequences are sampled from populations, and (2) a census that calibrates against a matched non-training document when the object is a single document (e.g., a book). We show that matched comparisons enable rigorous, calibrated memorization claims, and reveal where prior setups have validity issues. For instance, on Wikipedia OLMo 2 32B reproduces non-training 10-token suffixes roughly 24% as often as training ones: that share of the training generation rate reflects false positives, not memorization. For Llama 3.1 70B on books, the thresholds we calibrate are as low as 1e-27, supporting memorization claims for sequences that no feasible sampling budget would extract. Based on these results, we refine "extractable memorization" to require a valid memorization claim and near-certain generation within a realistic budget.

</details>


### [104] [Sample Efficient Generative Optimization for Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12488)
*Sarina Kopf,Cristina Nevado,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEGO是一个样本高效的分子优化框架，通过贝叶斯优化与自适应生成模型相结合，仅需其他方法十分之一的oracle调用量即可达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现、材料设计和催化中的分子优化需要在高评估预算限制下搜索巨大的化学空间，而高保真度oracle和实验测量成本高昂，因此方法的实际效果取决于其样本效率。

Method: SEGO框架将概率代理模型（对化学空间中可能命中区域形成假设）与生成模型（在该区域生成候选分子）相结合，通过采集函数选择最有希望的候选，并用oracle调用结果来优化代理模型和生成器。

Result: 在PMO基准上仅需其他方法十分之一的oracle调用量即达到SOTA性能；在多参数对接任务中，约用半数oracle调用量即可找到10个命中分子。

Conclusion: SEGO显著提升了分子优化的样本效率，使其更接近由直接实验反馈驱动的优化过程。

Abstract: Molecular optimization in drug discovery, materials design, and catalysis requires searching vast chemical spaces under tight evaluation budgets, since high-fidelity oracles and experimental measurements are costly. The practical impact of an optimization method therefore hinges on its sample efficiency: how few evaluations it needs to find strong candidates. We introduce Sample Efficient Generative Optimization (SEGO), a framework for Bayesian optimization on adaptively generated molecules. In SEGO, a probabilistic surrogate model forms a hypothesis about where hits lie in chemical space, a generative model is steered to propose candidates in that region, the most promising candidate is selected via an acquisition function, and the resulting oracle call is used both to sharpen the surrogate and to anchor the generator in real reward. SEGO attains state-of-the-art performance on the practical molecular optimization (PMO) benchmark using only one tenth of the oracle calls consumed by other methods, and on a multiparameter docking task it reaches ten hits in roughly half the oracle calls of existing approaches. These gains move molecular optimization closer to campaigns driven by direct experimental feedback.

</details>


### [105] [Adversarial Attacks on Online Handwriting using Salience-based Temporal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12500)
*Yataro Tamura,Brian Kenji Iwana,Jiseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于显著性的时序编辑攻击框架，通过在手写轨迹的时间步上插入/删除点而非添加噪声，生成对抗样本，在白盒和单次黑盒迁移设置下均有效，且保持手写视觉结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对在线手写识别的对抗攻击多基于图像域的加性空间扰动，会导致高频抖动和 unnatural 笔画伪影，不符合手写轨迹的时序特性。

Method: 利用梯度激活映射估计时间显著性，在显著时间步上插入或删除轨迹点，生成对抗样本，替代传统加性噪声扰动。

Result: 在Unipen和CASIA-OLHWDB数据集上，所提方法在单次黑盒迁移设置下优于传统图像域攻击，同时保持手写视觉结构完整。

Conclusion: 时序编辑攻击是对在线手写识别领域一种有效且相关的威胁模型，尤其适用于单次黑盒迁移场景。

Abstract: Deep learning models for online handwriting recognition have been shown effective and are increasingly deployed in practical applications. However, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks is still a challenge. Existing adversarial methods are predominantly designed for image-based inputs and typically rely on additive spatial perturbations. When applied to online handwriting, which is inherently represented as a time series of pen trajectories, such perturbations often introduce high-frequency jitter and visibly unnatural stroke artifacts. In this work, we propose a novel adversarial attack framework for online handwriting recognition based on salience-guided temporal editing. Instead of adding noise, the proposed method generates adversarial examples by inserting and deleting points at time steps selected according to temporal salience, preserving the shape and smoothness of the original handwriting. Temporal salience is estimated using gradient-based activation mapping, which guides edits toward time steps that strongly support the original class prediction. We evaluate the proposed approach on the Unipen and CASIA-OLHWDB datasets under both white-box and one-shot black-box attack settings. Experimental results demonstrate that while conventional image-based attacks achieve strong white-box performance, they exhibit poor transferability across models. In contrast, the proposed temporal editing attack achieves stronger one-shot black-box transferability while preserving the visual structure of the handwriting. These results indicate that temporal editing is a relevant threat model for online handwriting recognition, particularly in one-shot black-box transfer settings.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Mechanistic Reasoning for Chemical Reactions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12771)
*Xingyu Dang,Haocheng Tang,Junmei Wang,Yanjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集，并建立了FukuyamaBench基准来评估大语言模型在有机反应机理层次推理上的性能。微调的Qwen3-30B-A3B在精确路径匹配上达到8.3%，超过专用FlowER模型（5.1%），证明机理感知训练能显著增强化学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学大语言模型主要关注粗粒度的名称反应进行产物预测和逆合成分析，常导致物理不一致性和幻觉；而专用小规模机理推理生成模型在多样化化学空间上的泛化能力受限。因此需要构建大规模反应机理推理数据集并建立严格的基准来提升和评估LLMs的化学推理能力。

Method: 构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集；建立了FukuyamaBench基准（源自福山透《高等有机反应机理》），用于评估层次化机理推理性能；对Qwen3-30B-A3B模型进行微调训练。

Result: 微调的Qwen3-30B-A3B在FukuyamaBench Set A上达到8.3%的精确路径匹配率，超过专用FlowER模型（5.1%）。

Conclusion: 机理感知训练能显著增强大语言模型的化学推理能力，为提升LLMs在化学领域的推理可靠性提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Reaction mechanisms consist of the step-by-step sequences of elementary reactions that explain chemical transformations. Learning the mechanism logic is therefore essential for enhancing the fundamental chemical intelligence of large language models (LLMs). The stepwise deduction of reaction mechanism aligns naturally with the reasoning paradigms of reasoning LLMs. However, current chemical LLMs primarily emphasize coarse-grained name reactions for product prediction and retrosynthesis, often leading to physical inconsistencies and hallucinations. In contrast, specialized small-scale generative models for mechanism inference typically suffer from restricted generalization capacity across diverse chemical spaces. To overcome these limitations, we built a novel, large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms. Furthermore, we established the FukuyamaBench, a difficult benchmark derived from Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book, to rigorously evaluate model performance on hierarchical mechanism reasoning. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves 8.3% exact pathway match on FukuyamaBench Set~A, surpassing the specialized FlowER model (5.1%), demonstrating that mechanism-aware training substantially enhances chemical reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [107] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Forward-Forward算法中启发式设计（平方 goodness 和层间归一化）在生成模型下的统计基础：平方 goodness 是标度差异下似然比检验的充分统计量，层间归一化需移除长度保留坐标能量以避免深度坍缩，白化 goodness 可消除尺度膨胀捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FF算法的平方 goodness 和层间归一化通常被视为启发式设计，本文旨在揭示它们在一个显式生成模型下的统计合理性。

Method: 将FF算法置于显式生成模型框架下，通过似然比检验解释平方 goodness 的统计角色；推广到各向异性（马氏距离）和重尾分布（饱和统计量）；分析层间归一化的几何性质，指出其必须移除长度保留坐标能量以避免深度坍缩，并发现成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化 goodness 可消除。

Result: 平方 goodness 是零均值尺度差异下似然比检验的充分统计量；各向异性导出马氏 goodness；重尾分布导出饱和统计量，其斜率为后验精度（除性归一化）；层间归一化需去除长度保留坐标能量，否则出现深度坍缩；成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化可消除。

Conclusion: FF算法的启发式设计具有坚实的统计基础，揭示了其内在动机和局限性，并为改进算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.

</details>


### [108] [OOD-RL-Bench: A Benchmark Framework for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12523)
*Emil Mittag,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OOD-RL-Bench，一个用于评估强化学习轨迹中异常检测器的标准化框架，实验发现观测扰动和状态切换较易检测，而观测延迟和动作依赖的动态异常仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测基准主要针对图像分类器或静态低维数据集，未能考虑RL轨迹中复杂的、动作依赖的时间结构，缺乏专门评估RL场景下OOD检测器的标准化框架。

Method: 构建OOD-RL-Bench框架，通过共享接口和配置集成检测器与异常注入器，支持新评分方法和扰动家族的无修改评估；采用Deep Q-Network策略在LunarLander-v3环境中，使用匹配时间AUROC、AUPRC、假阳性率、检测延迟和分段发病指标评估检测器性能。

Result: 观测扰动和状态切换被多种方法高精度识别；观测延迟和动作条件动态即使将发病后异常分数与同一时间步的干净分数对比，仍然难以检测；各检测器在不同异常类型间表现出显著的性能差异。

Conclusion: OOD-RL-Bench为RL轨迹中的OOD检测提供了标准化的可扩展评估框架，揭示了当前检测方法在复杂异常类型上的不足，并为社区提供了可复现的基准资源。

Abstract: Reliable reinforcement learning (RL) agents must maintain operational integrity amidst sensor malfunctions, dynamic disturbances, and slow environmental shifts. The detection of out-of-distribution conditions is pivotal to determining when an agent's observations, transitions, or trajectory dynamics deviate from the assumptions underpinning its policy training. Current out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks typically evaluate image classifiers or static low-dimensional datasets, failing to account for the complex, action-dependent temporal structure inherent in RL trajectories. To address this gap, we present OOD-RL-Bench, a comprehensive and extensible framework designed to evaluate OOD detectors against categories of anomalies injected into RL trajectories. Detectors and anomaly injectors are integrated through shared interfaces and configuration, which allows new scoring methods and perturbation families to be evaluated without modification of the core benchmark loop. We evaluate the utility of the framework using a Deep Q-Network policy within the LunarLander-v3 environment. We assess the performance of each detector across a suite of anomaly types using matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, and segmented-onset metrics. Our analysis reveals significant performance variance across anomaly types: observation perturbations and regime switches are identified with high accuracy by several methods, while observation delay and action-conditioned dynamics remain difficult even when post-onset anomaly scores are compared against clean scores from the same timesteps. We make the framework, trained policy checkpoint, and complete results publicly available as a reproducible artefact.

</details>


### [109] [From Preimage Search To Source-Grounded Feature Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12526)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于源约束的特征反转方法，通过修复反向传播中的伴随信号，利用维纳滤波器从源样本的局部网络几何中重建输入域可视化，无需逐查询优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征反转方法仅匹配目标特征，但忽略了生成该特征的源样本信息，导致反演不唯一；需要一种既能匹配目标特征又能保留源样本局部几何信息的可解释性方法。

Method: 在计算图边界处，利用反向传播提供正确的反向依赖关系但修复伴随信号：首先通过均值种子VJP到上游状态的闭式矩阵维纳映射，再对JVP前向一致性残差进行第二次维纳映射，将修复后的状态沿同一DAG在一次有限反向传播中组合。该方法支持新输入、深度、通道及通道组，无需查询特定优化。

Result: 通过匹配目标与源控制实验验证了每个反演依赖于所选特征和源样本的局部算子而非目标无关的图像模板。预测条件特征图谱将这些可视化与对应内部特征的独立干预结果对齐。

Conclusion: 源约束特征反转能够在单个层和通道级别打开模型的隐藏特征层次结构，将网络从输入中提取的特征与塑造其决策的内部证据联系起来。

Abstract: Interpreting a neural network requires understanding what its internal features extract from a particular input. Feature inversion seeks to express a selected feature in the input domain, but canonical iterative methods search for an input whose re-encoded representation matches the target. Because many inputs can satisfy this constraint, target matching alone does not specify the inverse associated with the sample that generated the feature. We formulate source-grounded feature inversion by conditioning the inverse on the source-local network geometry at the target-generating input. At each boundary of the computational DAG, backpropagation provides the correct reverse dependencies but transports an adjoint signal rather than an upstream-state estimate. We locally repair this signal with a closed-form matrix Wiener map from a mean-seed VJP to the upstream state, followed by a second Wiener map for the JVP forward-consistency residual, and compose the repaired states through the same DAG in one finite reverse pass. One calibrated zero-intercept map family supports new inputs, depths, channels, and channel groups across diverse CNN and Transformer architectures, tensor components, and visual distributions without query-specific optimisation. Matched target and source controls verify that each inverse depends on the selected feature and the local operators of the sample being explained, rather than a target-independent image template. Prediction-conditioned feature atlases align these visualisations with independent interventions on the corresponding internal features. Together, source-grounded feature inversion opens the model's hidden feature hierarchy to inspection at the level of individual layers and channels, linking what the network extracts from an input to the internal evidence that shapes its decision.

</details>


### [110] [Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12829)
*Daehoon Gwak,Minhyung Lee,Junwoo Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的延迟分解框架，用于分析扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在并行生成中的推理效率，并从算法、架构/系统和推理时缩放三个维度系统梳理了加速技术，最后提供了可重复基准测试的指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型虽然理论上具备并行生成的优势，但实际部署中推理效率成为瓶颈。现有研究缺乏统一的框架来解耦算法、架构和系统层面的因素对端到端延迟的影响，导致加速技术难以进行严格比较。

Method: 提出统一的延迟分解框架，将dLLM推理延迟拆解为可独立分析的因子；沿三个分类轴（算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时缩放）对现有加速技术进行系统梳理与归类。

Result: 建立了dLLM推理延迟的分解框架，系统分类并评述了算法、架构/系统和推理时缩放三方面的加速技术，提供了可重复基准测试的指南。

Conclusion: 并行生成的理论优势在实际中需要依赖感知扩散的缓存与复用等推理机制才能转化为效率提升；现有加速技术被统一框架归类，但仍有开放挑战需要解决。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.

</details>


### [111] [Lightweight Multi-Scale Anomaly Detection for Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12599)
*Raheen Junaid Wani,Smruti R. Sarangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级多尺度自编码器(LMSAE)用于单变量时间序列异常检测，通过离散小波变换提取多尺度特征并结合多尺度损失函数，在小于500KB的模型大小下实现竞争性检测性能，在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上推理延迟降低9倍、功耗降低2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在IoT和边缘设备上计算开销大，难以在严格资源约束下运行，且缺乏多尺度敏感性导致难以捕获细微异常。

Method: 提出轻量级多尺度自编码器(LMSAE)，采用离散小波变换(DWT)提取多尺度特征，并设计多尺度损失函数增强对细微或隐藏异常的敏感性。

Result: 模型大小低于500KB，参数量显著减少；在基准数据集上达到竞争性或更优的检测性能；在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上推理延迟降低9倍，功耗降低2倍。

Conclusion: LMSAE在保持高检测精度的同时实现了极低的资源消耗，非常适合边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is increasingly important in IoT systems, sensor networks, and edge monitoring applications, where models must operate under strict constraints on memory, latency, and power consumption. While recent deep-learning approaches have improved detection accuracy, many remain computationally expensive and often fail to capture subtle anomalies due to limited multi-scale sensitivity. Autoencoders are widely used for anomaly detection because they reconstruct normal patterns well, leading to elevated reconstruction errors for anomalous inputs. Their simplicity and efficiency also make them suitable lightweight backbones for handling multi-scale inputs. To address these challenges, we propose a Lightweight MultiScale AutoEncoder (LMSAE) network for univariate time-series anomaly detection, designed to be compact and computationally efficient. LMSAE leverages the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to extract multi-scale features and employs a multi-scale loss function to improve sensitivity to subtle or hidden anomalies. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive or superior detection performance despite using significantly fewer parameters and a model size of less than 500 KB. LMSAE also achieves low-latency, low-power inference on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano, with 9x reduction in inference latency and 2x reduction in power consumption, making it ideal for edge deployment.

</details>


### [112] [The Geometry of Memorization: Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity as a Diagnostic for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12616)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FTSS指标g(t)，通过跟踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值来揭示连续时间生成框架中的流形几何结构，发现过拟合会导致频谱坍塌，并基于此开发了无需外部查询的内部几何审计方法来检测生成记忆化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成框架理论上偏好直线轨迹，但经验网络会发展出复杂的路径形变，现有方法难以有效检测生成模型中的记忆化问题。

Method: 提出Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity (FTSS) g(t)，一种无梯度、前向传递的指标，通过跟踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值来揭示流形几何结构，作为稳定秩的连续代理。

Result: 发现数据稀缺下泛化模型保持稳定有效维度，而过拟合模型出现频谱坍塌。

Conclusion: 基于g(t)开发了内部几何审计框架，利用纯内部轨迹动力学检测生成记忆化，无需外部成员查询或基线数据比较。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative frameworks construct probability paths between base and target domains by optimizing time-dependent velocity fields. While theoretical targets favor straight trajectories, empirical networks develop complex path deformations. This paper presents the Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity (FTSS) g(t), a gradient-free, forward-pass metric that exposes flow geometry by tracking the root-mean-square singular value of the state-transition matrix. Serving as a continuous proxy for stable rank, g(t) reveals a distinct geometric pathology under data scarcity: while generalizing models maintain stable effective dimensions, overfitting causes a spectral collapse. We leverage this structural phenomenon to develop an internal geometric audit based on g(t). Our framework detects generative memorization using purely internal trajectory dynamics, removing the need for external membership queries or baseline data comparison.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Forced Multibody Dynamics on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12627)
*Martine Dyring Hansen,Marta Ghirardelli,Elena Celledoni,David Martin de Diego,Brynjulf Owren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于离散强迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程在Lie群上仅利用位置数据学习机械系统动力学的架构，该方法直接在流形值构型空间上建模，尊重系统几何结构并保持几何不变性与守恒律，适用于多体系统及外部控制输入，在合成和真实数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要速度数据或忽略系统的几何结构，导致在速度数据不可用或含有噪声时性能下降，且难以保持物理系统的几何不变性与守恒律。

Method: 基于离散强迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程在Lie群上建模，仅利用位置数据直接在流形值构型空间上学习动力学，通过离散几何方法自然保持系统的几何结构。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上均表现出强劲性能，能够扩展到多体系统并容纳外部控制输入，且仅依赖位置测量即可有效学习。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何感知学习方法在仅使用位置数据的情况下，能够有效学习机械系统动力学并保持几何结构，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for learning the dynamics of mechanical systems based on discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations on Lie groups using only position data. By formulating the dynamics directly on manifold-valued configuration spaces, the method naturally respects the geometric structure of the systems and preserves geometric invariants and conservation laws. The reliance on position measurements alone makes the framework applicable in settings where velocity data are unavailable or noisy. The approach extends naturally to multibody systems, accommodates external control inputs, and demonstrates strong performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [114] [AdaPCLA: Adaptive Prior-Calibrated Logit Adjustment for Long-Tailed Longitudinal EHR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12645)
*Shuai Cui,Chen Wenxuan,Wenjie Du,Jian Lou,Dan Li,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaPCLA框架通过数据分布感知的训练策略（模拟退火训练）和零样本分布控制，使生成模型能够自适应拟合和生成纵向电子健康记录数据，在尾事件共现结构、下游效用和零样本跨群体适应方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归模型在生成纵向电子健康记录数据时，倾向于欠表示尾事件（如罕见疾病、症状）的共现结构，降低了罕见亚群生成数据的保真度和忠实性，亟需一种能够自适应处理长尾分布的生成方法。

Method: 提出AdaPCLA框架，包含两个核心机制：1）数据分布感知训练策略，通过模拟退火训练内部化数据知识参数；2）零样本分布控制，实现无需训练即可适应不同临床群体的生成。理论上通过标签经验神经正切核（NTK）刻画罕见码的逻辑更新，并推导了先验内化界以解释退火速度与NTK条件数对保留先验信号的影响。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV真实数据上，AdaPCLA在尾事件对召回率（TailPairSeen）上分别比HALO提升114.2%和65.1%；在零样本跨群体适应中，F1分数比GPT式生成提升3.5%。

Conclusion: AdaPCLA通过分布感知训练和零样本控制有效提升了罕见事件生成的保真度，理论分析与实验验证了一致性，为隐私保护下的纵向EHR生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative modeling of longitudinal Electronic Health Records is increasingly important for privacy-preserving research, yet standard autoregressive models tend to underrepresent the co-occurrence structure of tail events (i.e., diseases, symptoms), reducing the fidelity and faithfulness of generated data for rare subpopulations. To this end, we propose AdaPCLA framework, which enables generative models to adaptively fit and generate EHR data through a data distribution-aware training strategy; this is achieved by internalizing data knowledge parameters by simulated annealing training. It also supports training-free adaptation to a diverse clinical population for generation through zero-shot distribution control. Moreover, our theoretical analysis characterizes rare-code logit updates through the label-wise empirical NTK and derives a prior-internalization bound for how annealing speed and NTK conditioning affect retained prior signals. Experiments on real-world data show that AdaPCLA achieves consistent gains in tail plausibility, downstream utility, and zero-shot control; in particular, it improves TailPairSeen over HALO by 114.2% on MIMIC-III and 65.1% on MIMIC-IV, outperforms GPT-style generation by 3.5% F1 for zero-shot cross-population adaptation.

</details>


### [115] [Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning: A Path Toward Eliminating LLM Hallucination in Empirical Inference via Tool-Attested Kernel Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12650)
*Junyu Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EG-VAR 是一个基于 Lean 4 的架构，通过将 Lean 内核作为验证声明的唯一铸造者，确保每个验证输出都源自 attested 工具调用和内核检查的有效推理链，从而消除 LLM 输出中无证据支持的声称。在 TableBench 数值推理子集上达到 120/120，反事实压力测试中保持 100% 源忠实性，语义形式化误差仅为 1.7%-3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠工具访问无法使 LLM 经验推理可治理：已接受的输出可能并非源自 attested 证据，已接受的推理也可能经不起形式化审查。需要一种架构确保推理结果的证据基础和推理链的可审计性。

Method: 提出 EG-VAR 架构，基于 Lean 4 实现。Lean 内核通过工具证明公理和声明源提升机制，成为验证声明的唯一铸造者。每个验证输出必须结构上源自 attested 工具调用（定理 3.1）和内核检查的有效推理链（定理 3.2）；剩余输出以 Abstain 形式呈现并附带可重放的审计追踪。

Result: 在 TableBench 数值推理子集（n=120）上达到 120/120，而基线为 95%；在反事实压力测试（5 个领域 × 2 个模型）中保持 100% 源忠实性，相同工具基线降至 80-90%，无工具基线降至 50-80%。LLM 作为部署时形式化器的残余语义形式化误差：Sonnet 上 3.3%，Opus 上 1.7%。

Conclusion: EG-VAR 可作为高风险经验声明场景中的技术治理接口：形式化边车使目标命题、源范围、证据边界、证明义务和弃权条件均可审计，消除了当前无支持的验证输出，同时将形式化错误、源权威争议、歧义和弃权转化为明确的审计目标。未来，数据集、API、公共记录和 AI 生成文档中的类型化边车可将形式化负担分摊为可重用基础设施。

Abstract: Tool access alone does not make LLM empirical reasoning governable: accepted outputs need not descend from attested evidence, and accepted deductions need not hold up under formal scrutiny. We present EG-VAR (Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning), a Lean 4-based tool-calling architecture in which the Lean kernel is the sole minter of Verified claims via tool-attestation axioms and declared source lifts. Every verified output structurally descends from an attested tool call (Thm. 3.1) and a kernel-checked chain of valid inference (Thm. 3.2); residual outputs are honest Abstain with a replayable audit trail. On a subcollection of TableBench numerical reasoning (n=120), EG-VAR attains 120/120 versus a 95% same-tool baseline; on counterfactual stress tests (5 domains x 2 models), EG-VAR stays 100% source-faithful while same-tool drops to 80-90% (no-tool 50-80%). With the LLM as deployment-time formalizer, residual semantic-formalization error is 3.3% on Sonnet and 1.7% on Opus. We position EG-VAR as a technical-governance interface for high-stakes empirical claims: a formal sidecar makes the target proposition, source scope, evidence boundary, proof obligation, and abstention condition auditable, eliminating unsupported Verified outputs today while turning formalization errors, lift and source-authority disputes, ambiguities, and abstentions into explicit audit targets. Over time, typed sidecars in datasets, APIs, public records, and AI-generated documents can amortize this formalization burden into reusable infrastructure.

</details>


### [116] [Learning-based Probabilistic Load Forecasting with Post-hoc and In-model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12730)
*Sarah Al-Shareeda,Gulcihan Ozdemir,Heung Seok Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能建筑负荷预测中，当推理阶段输入特征需要重建时，不确定性应放置在模型何处。通过比较模块化后验残差分位数方案与集成式分位数学习方案，发现不确定性放置依赖于骨干网络结构。集成分位数学习在TFT模型上表现最佳，MAPE为2.2-3.6%，RMSE为28-83W，区间宽度比模块化方案窄约5倍。重建输入会使分位数评分增加106%，表明模型无法自动吸收重建引入的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能建筑负荷预测模型通常在密集、多变量、高频数据上离线训练，但实际部署时只能提供每小时、特征有限的输入。缺失特征需要重建，其误差会传播到模型中。如果不反映这种输入不确定性，预测区间可能校准不良，影响需求响应调度。因此需要研究推理输入重建后不确定性应放置在何处。

Method: 开发了一个统一的前一天概率预测框架，对齐时间分辨率、重建不可用输入并推导因果特征。比较了两种方案：模块化后验残差分位数方案和集成式分位数学习方案。使用三种深度学习骨干网络：循环、混合循环和基于注意力的Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT），在相同输入、预测范围、预处理规则和训练预算下进行比较。

Result: 不确定性放置依赖于骨干网络结构。集成分位数学习在TFT上最可靠，MAPE为2.2-3.6%，RMSE为28-83W，区间宽度比模块化方案窄约5倍。Diebold-Mariano检验支持TFT排名。重建输入使分位数评分增加106%，而区间宽度几乎不变。与非深度学习基线及季节性保留周的稳健性检验支持该排名。

Conclusion: 当推理依赖于重建输入时，后验残差分位数方法存在局限性。集成分位数学习在TFT骨干网络上效果最佳，但模型无法自动吸收重建引入的不确定性，导致预测区间校准质量下降。

Abstract: Smart-building load forecasters are often trained offline on dense, multivariate, high-frequency data, but deployment may provide only hourly, feature-limited inputs. Missing features must then be reconstructed, and their errors can propagate through the model. If this input uncertainty is not reflected, prediction intervals may become miscalibrated, affecting demand-response scheduling. Our work examines where uncertainty should be placed once inference inputs are reconstructed. We develop a unified one-day-ahead probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns temporal resolution, reconstructs the unavailable inputs, and derives causal features, and we compare a modular post-hoc residual-quantile scheme with an integrated in-model quantile-learning scheme. The comparison uses three mid-scale Deep Learning (DL) backbones: recurrent, hybrid recurrent, and attention-based Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) models, under identical inputs, forecasting horizon, preprocessing rules, and training budgets. Results show that uncertainty placement is backbone-dependent. Integrated quantile learning is most reliable with the TFT, yielding 2.2-3.6% MAPE and 28-83W RMSE on the labeled test window, while producing intervals about 5x narrower than the modular intervals at the closest-to-nominal coverage level. Diebold-Mariano tests support the TFT ranking and the mixed behavior of the recurrent backbones. A reconstruction-sensitivity test shows that reconstructed inputs increase the Quantile Score (QS) by 106% while interval width remains nearly unchanged, indicating that the model does not automatically absorb reconstruction-induced uncertainty. Robustness checks against non-DL baselines and seasonal hold-out weeks support this ranking. Our results expose the limits of post-hoc residual quantiles when inference depends on reconstructed inputs.

</details>


### [117] [What Makes a Representational Prior Work? Feature Families, Label-Free Invariances, and Critical Windows in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12735)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比先验促进泛化的条件，通过188次新实验验证了先验在特征族、监督方式、时机和设置四个维度上的作用机制，发现无标签的不变性先验可在早期窗口内实现可靠加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解grokking延迟的因果机制——即形成任务结构化表征所需的时间——并探究如何通过对比先验注入这种结构以加速泛化。

Method: 沿四个轴（先验内容、监督形式、施加时机、实验设置）进行188次新实验，系统分析对比先验的工作条件，包括使用错误特征族（幅度带）的先验、无标签不变性先验（仅交换对）、早期窗口施加等。

Result: (1) 来自错误特征族的先验会阻止泛化，与随机划分无显著差异；(2) 完全无标签的不变性先验在15/15次运行中泛化，中位数加速2.7倍，比有监督先验更可靠；(3) 仅在前2000个epoch（预算的4%）施加先验即可达到10/10泛化且加速2.7倍；(4) 结构注入使权重范数延迟律指数降低约17倍。

Conclusion: 特征族对齐决定先验是否允许泛化，不变性内容足以在无标签下实现加速，早期短暂窗口即可捕获几乎所有收益。

Abstract: Companion work showed the grokking delay is causally the time to form task-structured representations, injectable via a contrastive prior. Here we characterize what makes such a prior work, across four axes, in 188 new runs. Content: a coherent, learnable prior built from the wrong feature family (magnitude bands) blocks generalization like a random partition (1/15 vs 0/20 grok; $p=0.43$ between them), confirming the companion's prediction that priors act at the level of the circuit's features. Supervision: a fully label-free invariance prior -- positives are commuted pairs $(a,b)\sim(b,a)$ only -- generalizes in 15/15 runs at a median $2.7\times$ speedup, more reliably than the label-supervised prior itself ($p=0.038$), and combined with a weight-norm clamp yields the strongest method we test (median $17\times$, 5/5) -- strongest meaning reliably fast: plain cross-entropy with a clamp matches this speed only at the exact critical norm, while the prior keeps it fast across the entire clamp range. Timing: the prior is only needed early -- applied solely during the first 2000 epochs (4% of budget) it generalizes 10/10 at $2.7\times$, beating continuous application (8/10, $1.25\times$) and a duration-matched later window ($2.1\times$). Setting: the dissociation replicates on modular multiplication and across depths and normalization variants, and a clamp sweep quantifies the companion's central claim: structure injection flattens the weight-norm delay-law exponent about 17-fold (plain cross-entropy slows $31\times$ per +10 norm units, a lower bound as higher cells are censored, versus $1.22\times$ with the prior). Honest boundary: tasks that generalize before memorizing have no delay to control. Feature-family alignment decides whether a prior permits generalization; invariance content suffices for acceleration without labels; a brief early window captures nearly all of the benefit.

</details>


### [118] [Constraint-Aware Aggregation for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Microgrid Energy Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12763)
*Usman Haider,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究面向分布式能源协调的约束感知联邦强化学习聚合方法，提出基于惩罚项的简单聚合规则 w_i ∝ R_i - αV_i，在 DairyGridEnv 基准和真实负载数据上，相比 FedAvg 显著降低违规并提升奖励，无需双优化或修改本地训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦强化学习聚合方法（如 FedAvg）不考虑系统级约束，导致全局行为不安全；需要在保护数据隐私的前提下实现约束感知的聚合。

Method: 提出将局部性能与估计约束违规相结合的服务端聚合规则，核心为惩罚项方法 w_i ∝ R_i - αV_i，无需双优化或修改本地训练；还探索了奖励-违规联合方案。

Result: 在 DairyGridEnv（多农场电池储能协调）基准及芬兰和德国 FIELD 真实负载数据上，惩罚项聚合相比 FedAvg 大幅降低违规并提升奖励；联合方案虽可调但稳定性较差。

Conclusion: 轻量级聚合策略可在保持标准通信协议的前提下，显著提升联邦强化学习中的经验安全性。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FedRL) enables coordination of distributed energy resources without sharing raw local data, but standard aggregation methods such as FedAvg do not account for system-level constraints, often leading to unsafe global behavior. In this work, we study constraint-aware aggregation for federated reinforcement learning in distributed energy coordination. We propose aggregation rules that incorporate both local performance and estimated constraint violation into the server-side update. Among these, a simple penalty-based rule, $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$, consistently provides the most reliable trade-off between reward and safety, without requiring dual optimization or modifications to local training. \textcolor{black}{We evaluate our approach on DairyGridEnv, a benchmark modeling multiple farms coordinating battery storage under stochastic demand and a shared grid capacity constraint, and further assess robustness using real load-driven demand profiles from Finland and the German FIELD dataset. Across multiple seeds, penalty-based aggregation substantially reduces violations while improving reward relative to FedAvg in both synthetic and real load-driven settings.} A combined reward-violation scheme exposes a tunable trade-off via $λ$, but is less stable. These results demonstrate that lightweight aggregation strategies can substantially improve empirical safety in federated reinforcement learning while preserving standard communication protocols.

</details>


### [119] [AVQ-Attention: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12789)
*Winfried van den dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AVQ Attention通过自适应分配码本容量，在保持O(MN)复杂度下实现了比固定码本VQ注意力更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制的O(N^2)复杂度是Transformer模型的计算瓶颈。VQ注意力虽将其降至O(MN)，但存在码本容量分配不合理的问题：高注意力区域可能被粗糙量化，而低注意力区域浪费了表示容量。

Method: 提出自适应向量量化（AVQ）注意力机制，从少量码字开始，在前向传播中识别最重要的码字，并用预学习的子码字对其进行细化，实现高注意力区域的细粒度量化和低注意力区域的粗粒度量化。利用自定义Triton内核在Flash Attention的分块计算范式内完成整个自适应细化过程。

Result: 在保持O(MN)复杂度的前提下，相比固定码本VQ注意力实现了更好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应码本分配策略能有效解决VQ注意力中码本容量分配不均的问题，在不增加计算复杂度的情况下提升模型性能。

Abstract: The $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of attention over $N$ tokens remains a computational bottleneck in transformer models. Vector-Quantized (VQ) attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ by representing keys with $M$ codewords, but applies uniform codebook capacity regardless of where attention mass concentrates: high-attention regions of key space may be coarsely approximated while low-attention regions waste representational capacity. We propose Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention, which adaptively allocates codebook capacity based on attention importance. Starting from a small set of codewords, our method identifies the most important codes during the forward pass and refines them with pre-learned child codewords, achieving fine-grained quantization where it matters most while maintaining coarse quantization elsewhere. We develop an implementation using custom Triton kernels that enables the full adaptive refinement process, including importance scoring, child codeword insertion, and parent contribution replacement, to be carried out within the tiled computation paradigm of Flash Attention with minimal overhead. Our approach maintains $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ complexity while achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to fixed-codebook VQ-attention.

</details>


### [120] [Verifier-Based Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Reasoning Models for Thermal Energy Storage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12856)
*Takumi Shioda,Kohei Terashima,Tatsuo Nagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对开源推理模型进行微调，使其在建筑热储能调度任务中达到接近动态规划最优解的排放表现（61.2 vs 60.8 kg-CO2），仅需30条训练提示即可学习到候选比较、前瞻和可行性检查等规划模式，且具备一定的鲁棒性和迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑需根据电网条件灵活调节冷却负荷，热储能（TES）可实现这一调节，但提前数小时的调度规划受限于储能约束。MPC和强化学习等方法难以跨建筑规模化部署，因此亟需一种可扩展且高效的调度方法。

Method: 将精确离线动态规划（DP）的动作值转化为每个候选动作的密集奖励信号，通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对开源推理模型进行强化微调（RFT），使其作为上层调度器，从文本状态和预测中输出每小时热泵设定点。

Result: RFT将模型排放从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO2，接近DP最优值60.8 kg-CO2；GPT-5（推理模型）接近DP和MPC，而GPT-4o（非推理模型）排放高于无储能基线。RFT主要稳定了候选比较、前瞻和可行性检查等规划模式，而不是创造新策略。在预测误差、未见过的TES条件及电池任务上具有一定鲁棒性和迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于DP的可验证奖励为将开源推理模型适应到建筑储能调度提供了一条实用路径，结果支持进一步开展更高保真度的建筑整体控制测试及城市级能源管理的可扩展验证器研究。

Abstract: Buildings are expected to shift cooling loads in response to grid conditions. Thermal energy storage (TES) enables this shift, but scheduling it well requires planning hours ahead under storage constraints. Model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning are difficult to scale across buildings. This study instead adapts an open-weight reasoning model through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We convert exact offline dynamic-programming (DP) action values into dense rewards for every candidate action. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) trains the model as an upper-level scheduler that outputs hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluation uses a deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark where exact DP is tractable and the optimum is known. RFT reduces the open-weight model's emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, close to the DP optimum of 60.8 kg-CO2. GPT-5 nearly matches DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, a non-reasoning LLM, produces higher emissions than the no-storage baseline, so inference-time reasoning appears important. Trace analysis shows that RFT mainly stabilizes observable planning patterns (candidate comparison, look-ahead, and feasibility checking) rather than creating a new strategy. Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.

</details>


### [121] [Energy-Based Physics-Informed Form Finding for Clustered Tensegrity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12888)
*Jing Qin,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的学习框架，用于解决张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测这一逆问题，通过将总势能最小化和本构关系融入训练目标，实现了平衡节点构型与内力分布的同时预测，实验证明了该方法在物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体结构的找形与物理属性预测是结构力学中的基础逆问题，面临几何与力耦合导致的强非线性、结构稳定性保障、约束条件（边界条件、对称性）强制执行以及传统方法对噪声和异常值缺乏鲁棒性等挑战。

Method: 提出一种基于能量的学习框架，将总势能最小化和本构关系作为训练目标的一部分，实现平衡节点构型与相关物理量（杆件内力、力密度）的同步预测，通过基于能量的物理损失函数提升物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率。

Result: 在张拉整体棱柱和着陆器系统上的数值实验表明，该方法具有可扩展的找形能力和准确的结构属性预测能力，展现了巨大的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该能量基学习框架有效解决了张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测中的非线性与鲁棒性问题，是一种可扩展、物理一致的解决方案。

Abstract: Tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction are fundamental inverse problems in structural mechanics, which aim to determine equilibrium configurations and internal force distributions. These problems are challenging due to strong nonlinearity arising from the coupling between geometry and forces, the need to ensure structural stability, and the enforcement of constraints such as boundary conditions and symmetry. Moreover, traditional methods often lack robustness to noise and outliers. This paper proposes an energy-based learning framework for clustered tensegrity form finding and physical property prediction. The proposed approach incorporates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective, enabling the simultaneous prediction of equilibrium nodal configurations and associated physical quantities, including member forces and force densities. By incorporating energy-based physical losses directly into the learning process, the framework improves physical consistency, robustness, and data efficiency. Numerical experiments on tensegrity structures, including prism and lander systems, show the great potential of the proposed approach and demonstrate its capability for scalable form finding and accurate prediction of structural properties.

</details>


### [122] [Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12916)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo损失函数，通过促进类内坍塌和类间对比来学习归一化且结构良好的表示，在OpenML-CC18表格数据集上取得与SOTA方法相当的竞争性能，并实现更紧致的类聚类和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有损失函数（如点回归和交叉熵）在学习归一化且结构良好的表示方面存在不足，具体表现为最优配置的初始化距离较远、梯度信息不足以及类表示坍塌的激励不够强。

Method: 提出CoCo损失函数，同时鼓励类内表示坍塌（intra-class collapse）和类间对比（inter-class contrast），允许神经网络逼近具有大角度类间间隔的几何最优嵌入。从理论上分析了CoCo与点回归、交叉熵等目标函数的关系。

Result: 在OpenML-CC18基准的多样化表格数据集上，CoCo取得与核SVM、随机森林、点回归和基于交叉熵的神经网络等SOTA方法竞争的性能。理论分析和实验均表明CoCo促进更紧致的类聚类和更快收敛。

Conclusion: CoCo损失函数是一种有效的学习判别性表示的目标函数，能在保持竞争性预测性能的同时实现更优的表示结构。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [123] [Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12928)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效随机预测器，在在线二进制序列校准问题中实现了期望校准误差 \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\)，突破了经典的 \(T^{2/3}\) 下界，通过将 SPR-Calibration 过程与外部 Blackwell 风格校正层结合来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破在线二进制序列校准问题中经典的 \(T^{2/3}\) 校准误差下界，进一步降低校准误差的阶数。

Method: 将 SPR-Calibration 过程（控制条件均值估计序列的校准误差）与外部 Blackwell 风格校正层（控制用代理序列近似真实结果时的额外误差）相结合，利用二次势参数和 SPR-Calibration 预测器的稀疏性来分析总校准误差。

Result: 实现了期望校准误差 \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\)，其中 \(\varepsilon>0\) 为常数，优于经典的 \(T^{2/3}\) 界。

Conclusion: 通过双层校正架构成功突破了校准误差的经典下界，展示了 SPR-Calibration 过程与 Blackwell 校正相结合的潜力。

Abstract: We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\).
  Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.

</details>


### [124] [The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13006)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于功率谱的可预测性指标无法衡量上下文（长回溯、检索增强、预训练模型）带来的超越二阶统计量的价值，因为相位随机化会破坏此类价值。作者提出覆盖率赤字（coverage deficit）作为无标签的诊断指标，通过对比线性预测与模拟预测的增益来捕捉超越谱结构的信息。在七个基准上验证：基于窗口键的检索价值在替代对中从+33%崩溃至-35%，而谱指标不变；基础模型的价值中超越线性部分同样崩溃；线性窗口的价值则保持不变。覆盖率赤字能预测超越谱价值的符号，而谱指标不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱基可预测性指标被错误解读为衡量“添加上下文是否有助于预测”，但谱指标仅反映二阶统计量，无法捕捉相位结构中蕴含的高阶信息（如检索增强和基础模型提供的价值）。这导致实践者基于谱指标做出错误的部署决策，亟需澄清这一混淆并提出替代诊断方法。

Method: 提出不可能性结果：任何仅由功率谱构建的指标在相位随机化下不变，而二阶以上价值会被破坏。构造谱和边际固定的替代对（surrogate pairs）进行控制实验。提出覆盖率赤字（coverage deficit），其主项衡量模拟预测（analog prediction）对线性预测的增益，以捕捉超越谱结构的信息。在七个基准数据集上对比谱指标与覆盖率赤字对预测器价值的预测能力。

Result: 1）窗口键检索（window-keyed retrieval）的价值在替代对中从+33%中位数骤降至-35%（p<10^{-40}），而所有谱指标冻结不变；2）基础模型的价值分裂为存活的三阶部分和崩溃的超越线性部分；3）长线性窗口的价值不受影响；4）留一数据集交叉验证显示，覆盖率赤字的主结构项能预测超越谱价值的符号，而谱指标则相反。

Conclusion: 谱基可预测性指标不能回答“添加上下文是否有价值”的问题，会误导部署决策。覆盖率赤字作为无标签、配置级的诊断工具，能有效区分二阶（线性）与超越二阶（非线性）的价值来源，为预测模型选择提供可靠依据。

Abstract: A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.

</details>


### [125] [TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13028)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Wei-Jer Chang,Zachary Lee,Tim Wang,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraZero是一个基于程序化生成和自对弈训练的高效驾驶模拟器，支持1.3M agent-steps/秒的仿真速度，仅从真实地图几何数据出发，通过完全强化学习从零训练出零样本泛化、在InterPlan长尾基准上达到第一、在val14上最安全的驾驶策略，同时作为仿真智能体在Waymo Open Sim Agents上超越其他无演示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模拟器在速度、真实性和多样性三者之间存在权衡：对象级模拟器速度慢，单机系统缺乏异构智能体、多动力学模型和交通规则约束，而日志数据难以覆盖安全关键的长尾场景。作者希望构建一个兼具大规模强化学习速度、真实世界地图结构保真度和无限场景多样性的统一训练框架。

Method: 1) 使用可配置C引擎在CPU上运行仿真、GPU上进行策略推理，通过零拷贝路径实现1.3M agent-steps/秒；2) 仅利用日志数据提取真实地图几何，在每张地图上随机填充规则驱动的路人和信号控制器，并随机化智能体动力学、奖励和尺寸，生成无限场景；3) 完全采用强化学习自对弈训练，无人类演示和推理时的备用规划器。

Result: 在InterPlan长尾基准上取得第一名，超越更大的学习型规划器；在val14常规驾驶基准上获得最佳碰撞和碰撞时间分数，为最安全的方法；在Waymo Open Sim Agents上优于其他无演示方法，与最强的参考锚定自对弈方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: TerraZero证明：仅通过纯强化学习自对弈、从真实地图几何和随机化场景出发，即可训练出在长尾安全场景和常规驾驶任务上均表现优异、且零样本跨城市/数据集泛化的驾驶策略；同一框架同时胜任驾驶策略和仿真智能体两个角色。

Abstract: Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.

</details>


### [126] [The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13031)
*Jorge Diaz Chao,Konpat Preechakul,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG
