### [180] [Exact Solutions to a Class of Constrained Optimal Control Problems via Lossless Convexification for Digital Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12109)
*Vaibhav Upadhyay,Siddhartha Ganguly,Debasish Chatterjee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类含环形控制幅值约束的非凸连续时间最优控制问题，提出了基于无损凸化技术与分段常数控制参数化的数值求解方法，在保证约束严格满足的同时获得精确解，并通过三自由度航天器着陆问题验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天航空应用中常遇到含环形控制幅值约束的非凸连续时间最优控制问题，现有方法难以同时兼顾解的最优性、约束的严格满足与数值计算的高效性，亟需建立一种可行的数值求解技术。

Method: 首先采用无损凸化技术将原非凸最优控制问题转化为凸问题，然后通过分段常数函数对控制空间进行有限参数化，建立了一种既能保证解的精确性又能确保紧时间区间上不可数无穷约束严格满足的数值方法。

Result: 在三自由度航天器着陆问题上验证了该方法的有效性，展示了其在实际航空航天制导与控制任务中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 所提方法为线性系统中一类带环形约束的非凸连续时间最优控制问题提供了一种数值可行的求解途径，具备实时航空航天应用的潜力。

Abstract: This article establishes a new numerically viable technique for solving a class of constrained, nonconvex, continuous-time optimal control problems (OCPs) for linear systems that commonly arise in aerial and aerospace applications. The lossless convexification technique is employed to translate the original nonconvex OCP with annular control magnitude constraints into a convex problem, and then by finitely parametrizing the control space with piecewise constant functions, an efficient numerical approach is established that guarantees exact solutions while ensuring the satisfaction of an uncountable family of constraints over a compact time interval. The effectiveness of the approach is demonstrated on a spacecraft landing problem involving three degrees of freedom (DoF), underscoring its potential for real-world aerospace guidance and control tasks.

</details>


### [181] [Body-fitted tracking of 2d open curves with a level set based mesh evolution method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12116)
*Charles Dapogny*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合显式网格划分与隐式水平集方法的新型数值算法，用于追踪二维空间中开放曲线的运动。该方法通过同时采用两种互补的曲线表示（显式网格化+隐式水平集函数），实现了精确的几何/力学计算与大幅形状变化之间的平衡。文中展示了该方法在涡旋片卷起模拟、各向异性长度优化（如增材制造中激光路径优化）及裂缝重建等物理不连续性问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理开放曲线的大幅形变运动时，纯显式方法受限于网格畸变，纯隐式水平集方法难以处理开放曲线拓扑。因此需要一种结合两者优势的新型数值算法。

Method: 在每个演化迭代中同时维护两种曲线表示：（1）显式网格化表示，将曲线作为计算域网格实体的子集进行划分；（2）隐式表示，利用两个辅助标量函数的负、零、正子集的代数组合来捕捉曲线（基于水平集方法的变体）。两种表示相互补充，交替使用。

Result: 成功应用于多个物理场景：涡旋片卷起运动模拟、基于各向异性长度优化的开放曲线形状优化（用于改进增材制造中的激光路径）、断裂集重建。

Conclusion: 该耦合表示策略有效结合了显式方法的计算精度与隐式方法的拓扑灵活性，为开放曲线的大形变运动追踪提供了实用数值方案。

Abstract: This article describes a novel numerical algorithm for tracking the motion of a collection of open curves in two space dimensions. The proposed strategy combines two complementary representations of these curves at each iteration of the evolution process: on the one hand, they are meshed explicitly, as a sub-collection of the entities of a mesh of the total computational domain. Concurrently, using a variant of the Level Set Method, they are captured implicitly as algebraic combinations of the negative, zero and positive subsets of two auxiliary scalar functions, defined on the whole ambient space. This coupling of representations allows to perform accurate geometric or mechanical computations on these curves, while leaving room for large evolution of their shape. After the description of its main numerical ingredients, several applications examples of this methodology are proposed, where it is used to simulate the motion of open physical discontinuities, such as a vortex sheet roll-up, and to optimize the shape of open-ended curves, e.g. with respect to their anisotropic length, with the aim to improve the trajectory of a laser acting on a powder bed in the context of additive manufacturing, or to reconstruct fracture sets.

</details>


### [182] [The Certification Limits of KS-Type Layer Relaxations: A Square-Root Ceiling and Its Breakdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12134)
*Guillaume Lecomte*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了可分离双线性层松弛的认证能力，证明了在一般条件下认证缺陷最多为O(√N)，并揭示了从无排除到线性排除再到平方根上限的重新进入相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Kuznetsov-Sahinidis型松弛在最佳情况下能够认证的缺陷上界，理解不同参数区域中松弛认证能力的根本限制。

Method: 通过设定单层成本γ=g(1)和每粒子有界参考值，在满足两个低能见证条件的系统中，严格证明认证缺陷N-ρ(N)的上界为√N量级，并显式给出依赖参考界和γ的界。通过构造反例和Lennard-Jones几何系统进行对比分析。

Result: 所有γ>-1/2的松弛，认证缺陷最多为O(√N)；在γ=-1/2时存在缺陷为N-1的构造；几何Lennard-Jones系统中三原子链恢复平方根上限，足够负的单例成本允许线性缺陷；低于稳定性常数时无松弛能排除任何跨度。

Conclusion: 这些区域定义了层松弛的认证谱，揭示了从无排除到线性排除再到普适平方根上限的重新进入相变，表明平方根上限在抽象见证类中是最优的。

Abstract: A separable bilinear layer relaxation assigns a population cost to each layer and a nonpositive bilinear coupling to each pair of layers, then excludes a profile span whenever the relaxed minimum exceeds a reference value. We ask what a Kuznetsov-Sahinidis-type relaxation can certify at best.
  Let gamma = g(1) be the singleton-layer cost. For every valid relaxation with gamma > -1/2, every reference bounded per particle, and every underlying system satisfying two elementary low-energy witness conditions, we prove that the certified deficit N - rho(N) is at most of order sqrt(N), with an explicit bound depending only on the reference bound and gamma. Thus no relaxation in this class can certify a larger asymptotic deficit, independently of the detailed layer cost, interaction kernel, or reference.
  The threshold is sharp in the abstract witness class: at gamma = -1/2 we construct a valid relaxation with a bounded sound reference and deficit N - 1. The geometric Lennard-Jones system behaves differently. A three-atom chain restores the square-root ceiling at the endpoint, while sufficiently negative singleton costs allow valid relaxations with linear deficit. For still lower costs, beyond the stability constant, no sound relaxation excludes any span.
  These regimes define a certification spectrum for layer relaxations and reveal a reentrant transition between no exclusion, linear exclusion, and the universal square-root ceiling.

</details>


### [183] [LQG solution for POMDP without estimating states: A minimum variance approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12135)
*Ranjan Sarkar,Prabhat K. Mishra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在不完全和损坏测量条件下离散时间LTI系统的控制问题，提出了一种无需显式状态估计的LQG控制器设计方法，利用最小方差对偶性将控制输入表示为可用测量和过去输入的线性函数，简化为确定性优化问题，并通过理论证明和数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LQG控制通常依赖显式状态估计（如卡尔曼滤波），但在测量不完整或损坏的情况下，状态估计质量下降，导致控制性能恶化。本文旨在探索一种无需显式状态估计的替代控制方案，以应对有缺陷的测量环境。

Method: 利用最小方差对偶性，将当前控制输入表示为可用测量和先前控制输入的线性函数，从而将随机最优控制问题转化为一个可解的确定性优化问题。

Result: 通过数值实验展示了所提方法在存在测量不完整和损坏情况下的实际控制性能，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的无显式状态估计的LQG控制框架在理论上成立，在实际应用中表现良好，为处理不完整和损坏测量条件下的控制问题提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the control of discrete-time linear time-invariant (LTI) systems subject to incomplete and corrupted measurements. Specifically, we focus on designing a Linear Quadratic Gaussian (LQG) controller without relying on explicit state estimation. By leveraging minimum variance duality, our approach allows the current control input to be represented as a linear function of available measurements and previously applied inputs, successfully reducing the task to a tractable deterministic optimization problem. We provide theoretical justification for this framework and demonstrate its practical effectiveness through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [184] [Designing Hierarchical Hub-and-Spoke Drone-Based Networks for Delivering Time-Sensitive Healthcare Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12198)
*Sayed Hamid Hosseini Dolatabadi,Tanveer Hossain Bhuiyan,Bo Zeng,J. Tyler Kennedy,Brian O Neill*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种分层枢纽-轮辐式无人机网络，用于配送具有不同释放时间和截止时间的时间敏感医疗物品（如全血和疫苗）到固定和移动目的地。通过异构无人机编队和多程配送模式，提出了混合整数规划模型以及定制化精确求解方法和快速启发式算法。基于俄勒冈州Pendleton的真实全血配送数据和实际无人机飞行测试数据，结果表明精确求解方法比Gurobi快31倍，启发式算法快3,375倍，且多程配送比单程效率高142.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地面配送系统在区域医疗网络中常面临响应速度慢（如交通拥堵）和运营效率低（如血液浪费）的问题，亟需利用无人机配送系统实现时间敏感医疗物品的快速高效递送。

Method: 建立了混合整数规划模型，同时决策移动配送目的地的选址/分配、无人机路径规划和调度；提出了结合问题特定重构和动态割平面的定制化精确求解方法；利用简化问题变体设计了快速启发式算法。

Result: 基于真实全血配送数据和实际无人机飞行测试，精确求解方法比Gurobi快31倍，启发式算法快3,375倍；多程配送比单程配送成本效率提高142.8%。

Conclusion: 所提出的无人机配送网络模型和求解方法能显著提升时间敏感医疗物品的配送效率，多程配送模式相比单程具有更高的成本效益，为区域医疗网络中的无人机物流提供了有效的决策支持。

Abstract: Timely distribution of medical items (e.g., whole blood and vaccines) across regional healthcare networks often requires high-volume delivery operations from central facilities (e.g., blood banks) to intermediate regional hospitals, followed by rapid last-mile deliveries to points of injury. Traditional ground-based delivery systems often suffer from limited responsiveness (e.g., traffic congestion) and operational inefficiencies (e.g., blood waste). Leveraging aerial-drone-based delivery systems offers a promising solution for the fast and efficient delivery of time-sensitive medical items across regional healthcare networks. Therefore, we study a hierarchical hub-and-spoke drone-based network for delivering time-sensitive medical items with distinct release and due times to fixed and mobile delivery destinations. We consider a heterogeneous fleet of drones with distinct characteristics (e.g., cost, battery capacity, and speed) and different multi-trip delivery modes. We propose an efficient mixed-integer programming model for location/allocation of mobile delivery destinations, as well as routing and scheduling drones to minimize the total investment and operational costs of the drone delivery network while maintaining the delivery due times. We develop a customized exact solution method integrating problem-specific reformulations and dynamic cutting planes, as well as a fast heuristic algorithm by leveraging a simplified problem variant. Results based on a real-life case study of whole blood delivery data from Pendleton, Oregon, United States, and actual drone flight test data demonstrate that our exact and heuristic solution methods are 31 and 3,375 times faster, respectively, than the Gurobi solver. Results also show that allowing drones to perform multiple trips is 142.8% more cost-efficient than single trips.

</details>


### [185] [Revisiting Simultaneous Methods for Dynamic Optimization in the GPU Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12201)
*Joseph W. Choi,Sungho Shin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了动态优化中求解DAE约束优化问题的序贯法与联立法，重点分析了GPU计算如何改变两者的效率。联立法利用GPU并行计算，在最大规模问题上相比序贯法实现了最高5.4倍加速，且GPU上的优势比CPU更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动态优化问题中序贯法和联立法的效率对比在GPU计算时代需要重新评估，以探索并行计算能否克服传统方法的瓶颈。

Method: 采用基于正交配点法的联立方法，在开源建模框架下应用于系统生物学参数估计基准问题，分别使用GPU和CPU求解器对联立形式进行求解，并与序贯法基线进行对比。

Result: 联立法在成功求解的最大规模问题上相比序贯法实现了最高5.4倍加速；联立法在GPU上的性能优势比CPU上更显著，但可靠性不如序贯法。

Conclusion: GPU计算显著增强了联立法在大规模DAE约束优化问题上的优势，但序贯法在处理刚性系统和自适应时间步长方面仍具可靠性优势。

Abstract: We revisit the classical topic in dynamic optimization: sequential vs simultaneous methods for solving DAE-constrained optimization problems, with a particular focus on how graphics processing unit (GPU) computing changes their effectiveness. Sequential methods offer key advantages through adaptive time stepping at the differential-algebraic equation (DAE) solver level, which is especially effective for handling stiff systems. However, long-time-horizon simulations remain a computational bottleneck, as time integration is inherently sequential and limits parallelization within the optimization algorithm. In contrast, simultaneous approaches are well-suited for parallel computing. They address these limitations by exploiting the highly repetitive structure of discretized DAE systems at the function evaluation level and leveraging sparse linear algebra routines that enable elimination tree-level parallelism. Although simultaneous methods typically lack adaptive time stepping, this limitation can often be mitigated by choosing a sufficiently fine initial mesh or iteratively adjusting mesh coarseness in an outer loop. In this work, we revisit the capabilities of the simultaneous approach in a GPU computing environment and assess its performance against a sequential method baseline. We employ a simultaneous approach based on orthogonal collocation within an open-source modeling framework and apply it to parameter estimation benchmarks from systems biology. We evaluate both GPU and CPU solvers on the simultaneous formulation. Our results show that, although less reliable, the simultaneous approach achieves up to 5.4x speedup compared to the sequential baseline among the largest instances where both methods solve successfully. The advantage of the simultaneous method with respect to problem size is more pronounced on GPUs than on CPUs.

</details>


### [186] [Structured Preconditioning in Affine-Invariant Geometry: Projection, Certificates, and Kronecker Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12286)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在仿射不变黎曼度量下，Kronecker正定矩阵的最近结构近似（仿射不变投影）与最佳结构预条件子（Hessian相对条件数最小化）之间的精确关系：当且仅当极端谱状态的张量边缘相同时，二者等价。文章还建立了可计算的边缘失配残差、维度最小严格分离例子及残差校准界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近结构近似与最佳结构预条件子是两种不同的矩阵优化问题，现有研究未明确它们之间的关系。本文旨在确定在Kronecker正定矩阵族下，这两种问题的精确联系。

Method: 采用仿射不变黎曼度量，利用Kronecker族的闭凸性和测地凸性定义唯一仿射不变投影；通过部分迹正规方程分析对数残差；建立条件最优性的谱边缘张量边缘相等条件；构造$2\times 3$反例实现维度最小严格分离；使用区间算术和向外舍入保证数值验证的可靠性。

Result: 证明了仿射不变投影是Hessian相对条件数最小化器当且仅当极端谱状态的张量边缘相同；边缘失配残差可判定最优性或产生严格下降方向；两个相对谱级强制投影最优性；所有$2\times 2$ Kronecker投影均为条件最优；$2\times 3$构造是维度最小反例；建立了残差校准界以界定最佳可达条件数和投影次优性。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了Kronecker正定矩阵框架下两种优化问题的等价条件，提供了可计算的判定准则和严格数值验证方法，并将经典对角/块Loewner sandwich、固定基原始-对偶障碍等纳入统一证书语言。

Abstract: Nearest structured approximation and best structured preconditioning solve different matrix optimization problems. We determine their exact relation for Kronecker positive-definite matrices under the affine-invariant Riemannian metric. The Kronecker family is closed and geodesically convex, so every full matrix has a unique affine-invariant projection. Its logarithmic residual satisfies partial-trace normal equations and yields certified point and objective errors for an Armijo projection solver. Our central result shows that this unique projection is also a minimizer of the Hessian-relative condition number if and only if the extreme spectral states admit identical tensor marginals. A computable marginal-mismatch residual either vanishes at a condition-optimal projection or produces a strict descent direction. Two relative spectral levels always force projection optimality; more strongly, every $2\times 2$ Kronecker projection is condition-optimal. An explicit $2\times 3$ construction is therefore a dimension-minimal strict separation. Residual-calibrated bounds further bracket the best attainable Kronecker condition number and the suboptimality of the projection. Supporting results place classical diagonal and block Loewner sandwiches, fixed-basis primal--dual obstructions, and general log-spectral targets in the same certificate language. Given validated numerical enclosures and outward-rounded comparisons, an interval-safe corollary preserves the soundness of the full Kronecker tests. Deterministic small-matrix checks, including a multistart generic log-factor oracle independent of the partial-trace solver, verify the stated identities and bounds.

</details>


### [187] [An adaptive interior-point method with backtracking line search for convex constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12318)
*Fadi Hamad,Oliver Hinder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非自和谐障碍设置的正则化牛顿法，用于求解带约束的凸优化问题，并实现了$\tilde{O}(ε^{-2/3})$的迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内点法在凸优化中理论保证依赖于自和谐障碍假设，但这一假设在实际问题中并不总是成立。现有方法对无约束凸优化研究充分，但对无自和谐假设的约束凸优化方法缺乏理论分析。

Method: 针对目标函数和约束具有三阶可微且一、二阶导数Lipschitz连续的条件，开发了一种结合线搜索的正则化牛顿法，应用于对数障碍函数。

Result: 从严格可行点出发，该方法在$\tilde{O}(ε^{-2/3})$次迭代内找到ε-近似最优解。

Conclusion: 所提方法在更弱的假设下为约束凸优化提供了理论保证，填补了非自和谐设置下内点法复杂度分析的空缺。

Abstract: Interior-point methods (IPMs) are widely adopted due to their high practical efficiency in solving linear, convex, and nonconvex optimization problems. For convex optimization, this performance is theoretically well-supported: there are strong complexity guarantees for self-concordant barrier setups \cite{nesterov1994interior}, which cover linear and conic optimization. On the other hand, unconstrained convex optimization is well-studied. However, there is limited analysis of constrained convex optimization methods without the self-concordance assumption. We develop and analyze a regularized Newton method with line search applied to the log barrier function in the setting that the objective and constraints are thrice differentiable and have Lipschitz continuous first and second derivatives. Starting from a strictly feasible point, our method finds an $ε$-approximately optimal solution in $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2/3})$ iterations.

</details>


### [188] [On the Singularity Degree of Shor Relaxations for 0-1 Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12476)
*Hao Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸二次优化中Shor松弛的奇异度。对于线性约束定义的二元可行集，Shor松弛的奇异度至多为1，且可通过求解LP而非SDP来恢复Slater条件；而对于二次等式约束，奇异度可随矩阵阶数线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor松弛是非凸二次优化的标准工具，但可能违反Slater条件。奇异度量化了这种退化程度，理解不同约束类型对奇异度的影响对于优化算法的设计和分析至关重要。

Method: 通过构造和分析不同约束类型（线性等式/不等式 vs 二次等式）下的二元可行集，研究其Shor松弛的奇异度。利用面缩减（facial reduction）步骤数来量化奇异度。

Result: (1) 对于线性约束定义的二元可行集，Shor松弛奇异度≤1，当LP松弛包含开立方体内严格满足所有不等式的点时奇异度为0，否则为1。(2) 对于每个n≥1，可构造一个由二次等式约束定义的二元可行集，其Shor松弛的奇异度为n（矩阵变量阶数为n+1）。

Conclusion: 线性约束下Shor松弛的奇异度一致有界（至多为1），且可以通过求解LP而非SDP来恢复Slater条件；但二次等式约束下奇异度可随矩阵阶数线性增长，表明两类约束在退化性质上存在本质差异。

Abstract: Shor relaxations are standard tools for nonconvex quadratic optimization, but they may fail Slater's condition. The singularity degree quantifies this degeneracy by the number of facial-reduction steps needed to reach the minimal face. We determine how the constraints defining a binary feasible set affect this quantity. For every nonempty binary set defined by linear equations and inequalities, the Shor relaxation has singularity degree at most one. It is zero precisely when the linear programming (LP) relaxation contains a point in the open cube that strictly satisfies every inequality, and is one otherwise. As an algorithmic consequence of this characterization, Slater's condition can be restored by solving an LP rather than an auxiliary semidefinite program (SDP). In sharp contrast, for every $n\geq1$, we construct a binary feasible set defined by quadratic equalities whose Shor relaxation has a positive semidefinite matrix variable of order $n+1$ and singularity degree $n$. Thus, singularity degree is uniformly bounded in the linearly constrained case but can grow linearly with the matrix order when quadratic defining constraints are used.

</details>


### [189] [Extension of the safeguarding stepsize interval in Adaptive Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12478)
*Saneatsu Kagawa,Nobuo Yamashita*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 受自适应梯度下降法启发，本文提出一种自适应步长区间方法，通过将Barzilai-Borwein步长投影到该区间以保证全局收敛并加速收敛，同时提出扩展该区间以更频繁地使用完整BB步长，并通过数值实验验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应梯度下降法虽能保证全局收敛，但未能充分利用Barzilai-Borwein步长的加速潜力，需要在保证收敛性与加速效果之间寻求更好的平衡。

Method: 基于AdGD思想构建自适应步长区间作为保证全局收敛的条件，将BB步长投影到该区间；进一步扩展该区间以更频繁地采用完整BB步长。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的结合自适应步长区间与BB步长的方法在保证全局收敛的同时实现了加速效果。

Conclusion: 通过自适应步长区间投影BB步长可兼顾全局收敛性与加速性能，扩展区间后能更充分地发挥BB步长的优势。

Abstract: In this paper, inspired by the idea of Adaptive Gradient Descent (AdGD) by Malitsky and Mishchenko, we propose a method that adaptively provides a stepsize interval, which represents the condition for stepsizes ensuring global convergence. By projecting the Barzilai Borwein stepsize onto this interval, we can ensure global convergence and expect further acceleration of convergence. Furthermore, we propose enlarging the stepsize interval obtained by AdGD and show that global convergence can be guaranteed within this enlarged stepsize interval. This extension of the interval enables us to adopt the full Barzilai-Borwein (BB) stepsize more frequently. We report the results of numerical experiments on the stepsize that combines the proposed interval with the BB stepsize.

</details>


### [190] [Semismooth Newton methods for degenerate polyhedral projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12551)
*Chao Ding,Fuxiaoyue Feng,Xudong Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对退化多面体投影问题的对偶半光滑牛顿法，利用对偶表示的非唯一性，通过构造原始-对偶提升投影等价集的极值点几何，在无需正则化的情况下恢复牛顿步所需的非奇异广义雅可比矩阵，并开发了具有局部超线性收敛和全局收敛的算法。数值实验验证了该方法在高度退化场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化多面体投影问题中，对偶残差的广义雅可比矩阵即使在解集附近也可能保持奇异，现有方法通常采用正则化处理，但会引入额外参数并影响收敛性能。本文旨在不依赖正则化的情况下恢复牛顿步所需的非奇异性条件。

Method: 1) 提出原始-对偶提升投影等价集，证明其始终存在极值点；2) 建立极值点几何与广义弱严格Robinson约束规范及满列秩条件的联系；3) 开发非精确对偶SSN方法（局部超线性收敛）及其全局化版本（单调代表元选择+Wolfe线搜索）。

Result: 全局收敛性证明，局部超线性收敛速率恢复，在正则化最优传输、电池调度可行性恢复、占据测度投影等高度退化问题上的数值鲁棒性验证。

Conclusion: 通过极值点几何而非正则化可恢复牛顿步所需的雅可比非奇异性，所提方法在高度退化设定下兼具全局收敛与快速局部收敛特性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study dual semismooth Newton (SSN) methods for degenerate polyhedral projection problems, where generalized Jacobians of the dual residual may remain singular even arbitrarily close to the solution set. Rather than regularizing these singular systems, we exploit the nonuniqueness of the dual representation. We introduce a primal--dual lifted projection-equivalent set that always possesses extreme points without additional structural assumptions on the polyhedron, and show that its extreme-point geometry identifies dual representatives at which nonsingular generalized Jacobians of the dual residual can be constructed. This geometry is further linked to a full-column-rank condition and a generalized weak strict Robinson constraint qualification, showing that the regularity required by the Newton step can be recovered rather than imposed \emph{a priori}. We also establish displacement bounds that connect representative selection throughout the algorithm with the local Newton mechanism. Building on this variational framework, we develop an inexact dual SSN method with local superlinear convergence and a globalized version combining monotone representative selection with a Wolfe line search. The resulting method is globally convergent and eventually recovers the fast local rate. Numerical experiments on regularized optimal transport, battery-scheduling feasibility restoration, and occupation-measure projection demonstrate its robustness in highly degenerate settings.

</details>


### [191] [Learning-enabled Acceleration of Scenario-based Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12775)
*Trinh Tran,Binh Nguyen,Truong X. Nghiem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习加速的ADMM算法，通过并行计算和Moreau包络学习高效求解场景模型预测控制（SBMPC）问题，在微电网能量管理任务中相比IPOPT和MadNLP实现了显著的计算加速，同时保持高控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBMPC虽然能显式处理不确定性，但其计算复杂度随场景数和预测时域快速增加，限制了其在实时规划与控制中的适用性。现有求解器难以兼顾计算效率与求解精度。

Method: 将SBMPC问题重构为共识形式，通过ADMM解耦场景相关动力学与非预测性约束，实现场景和时间步的并行更新；在此基础上引入学习到优化（learning-to-optimize）策略，利用Moreau包络学习代价函数来加速ADMM的原始更新步骤。

Result: 在微电网能量管理问题上，与IPOPT和MadNLP相比，所提方法在保持可靠闭环控制性能的同时，实现了显著的计算加速。

Conclusion: 学习加速的ADMM算法能有效解决SBMPC的计算瓶颈，使其更适用于实时规划与控制场景。

Abstract: Scenario-based model predictive control (SBMPC) is a variant of model predictive control (MPC) that explicitly accounts for uncertainty by optimizing control actions over multiple predicted scenarios. However, its computational complexity increases rapidly with the number of scenarios and prediction horizon, limiting is applicability to real-time planning and control. This paper presents a learning-accelerated Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) algorithm for efficiently solving SBMPC problems by leveraging parallel computing and Moreau envelope learning, while maintaining high solution accuracy. We reformulate the SBMPC problems into consensus forms that can be decomposed via ADMM, separating the scenario-dependent dynamics from non-anticipativity constraints and enabling parallel updates across scenarios and time steps. Building on this decomposition, we utilize existing learning-to-optimize schemes, which leverages Moreau envelope learning of the cost function to accelerate the primal update in ADMM, thereby reducing computation time. The proposed framework is evaluated on a microgrid energy management problem subject to load and renewable generation uncertainties. Comparisons with IPOPT and MadNLP, popular and modern nonlinear programming solvers, demonstrate substantial computational speedups while maintaining reliable closed-loop control performance.

</details>


### [192] [Real and complex stability radii for a class of transport networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12812)
*Anthony Hastir,Birgit Jacob,Hannes Wagener*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类边界控制、边界观测的双曲型偏微分方程，刻画了其稳定性与鲁棒性，证明了渐近稳定与指数稳定等价，给出了实/复稳定半径的显式公式，并讨论了其对偶系统在可控性与可观性中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立边界控制、边界观测的双曲型PDE系统的稳定性理论，并量化其鲁棒性，同时探讨稳定性与对偶系统可控性/可观性的联系。

Method: 采用算子半群理论与频域方法，分析稳定性的等价条件；通过稳定半径公式量化鲁棒性；利用对偶系统关联可控性与可观性。

Result: 证明了渐近稳定与指数稳定在该类系统中等价；导出了实与复稳定半径的显式表达式；通过对偶系统建立了可控性与可观性的联系。

Conclusion: 边界控制/观测的双曲型PDE具有明确的稳定性等价关系与可计算的鲁棒性指标，且对偶框架为可控性与可观性提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We characterize stability and its robustness for a class of boundary controlled, boundary observed hyperbolic partial differential equations. In particular, we show that asymptotic and exponential stability coincide for this class. Furthermore, we introduce the real and complex stability radii for which we give explicit formulas. In addition, we consider the dual system and its application to controllability and observability. Our main results are illustrated with two examples.

</details>


### [193] [From Manifold Identification to Newton Acceleration on Intersections: Sparse Stiefel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12877)
*Shixiang Chen,Wen Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Stiefel流形上的稀疏复合优化问题提出了MIX方法，通过扰动策略处理非横截情况下的几何困难，在保证全局收敛的同时实现了局部超线性收敛，并在压缩模态和稀疏PCA任务中显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Stiefel流形上的稀疏复合优化中，非光滑正则化子识别的活动流形与Stiefel流形可能非横截相交，导致无法直接应用黎曼牛顿步，现有方法缺乏对该几何困难的理论处理与高效算法。

Method: 1) 在横截情形下证明ManPG切近邻映射的局部识别性质；2) 对非横截情形引入离对角扰动Stiefel族恢复识别几何，并提供可验证的支撑级条件用于清洁相交；3) 提出MIX方法，将ManPG与牛顿-CG结合，在移动识别交集上执行带保护的优化。

Result: 在一般清洁相交设定下证明了全局下降性与KKT残差保证；在横截或扰动情形下，结合序列收敛与二阶充分条件，证明有限步识别活动流形后局部超线性收敛；数值实验在压缩模态和稀疏PCA上显著提升效率且不牺牲解质量。

Conclusion: MIX方法有效克服了Stiefel流形上稀疏复合优化的几何障碍，在理论上统一了横截与非横截情形的收敛性分析，实践中展现了优越的计算性能。

Abstract: We study Newton acceleration for sparse composite optimization on the Stiefel manifold. The main difficulty is geometric: the active manifold identified by the nonsmooth regularizer may fail to intersect the Stiefel manifold transversely, which obstructs a Riemannian Newton step on the identified manifold. In the transverse case, we prove local identification of the ManPG tangent proximal mapping. For nontransverse cases, we introduce an off-diagonally perturbed Stiefel family that generically restores the identification geometry while yielding an \(O(\|Δ\|_F)\)-KKT guarantee for the original problem. We also derive verifiable support-level conditions for clean intersection, which cover nontransverse sparse patterns and yield the smooth moving local models used by the Newton correction. Based on these results, we propose MIX, a safeguarded ManPG/Newton-CG method on moving identified intersections. In the general clean-intersection setting, we prove global descent and KKT-residual guarantees for MIX. In the transverse or generically perturbed cases, we further show that, under sequence convergence and second-order sufficient conditions, MIX identifies the active manifold in finite time and then converges locally superlinearly. Numerical experiments on compressed modes and sparse PCA show that MIX substantially improves efficiency while preserving solution quality.

</details>


### [194] [Host-Parasitoid Dynamics and Biological Control of the Sugarcane Borer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12878)
*Fredson Aguiar,M. Soledad Aronna,Roberto Guglielmi,Beatriz Laiate,Alexandre Molter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个六维宿主-寄生蜂动力学模型，研究两种寄生蜂（卵寄生蜂Trichogramma galloi和幼虫寄生蜂Cotesia flavipes）联合防控甘蔗螟虫Diatraea saccharalis的策略。模型采用Holling II型功能响应，分析了平衡态结构、灭绝平衡点的局部稳定性以及分岔特性。针对害虫种群超过经济损害阈值的情况，设计了三种控制策略：开环最优控制、SDRE反馈控制和基于Lyapunov的脉冲反馈控制。数值仿真表明三种策略均能将害虫控制在阈值以下，其中脉冲策略以显著更少的寄生蜂释放次数实现有效抑制，最具实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两种寄生蜂联合防控甘蔗螟虫的生物防治策略，解决传统模型未耦合卵期和幼虫期动态、未考虑寄生饱和效应的问题，并针对害虫种群超过经济损害阈值设计可行的主动控制方案。

Method: 建立六维宿主-寄生蜂模型，采用Holling II型功能响应描述寄生交互，耦合卵期和幼虫期动态；通过平衡态分析和分岔分析揭示系统动力学特性；分别采用开环最优控制、SDRE反馈控制和基于Lyapunov的脉冲反馈控制设计三种生物防治策略，并进行数值仿真对比。

Result: 三种控制策略均能将害虫种群维持在经济学损害阈值以下；脉冲反馈控制策略相比连续控制策略，所需寄生蜂释放次数显著减少，具有最高的实际应用可行性。

Conclusion: 脉冲反馈控制策略是甘蔗螟虫生物防治中最具实践价值的方案，能以最少的寄生蜂释放实现有效的害虫抑制。

Abstract: We investigate biological pest control strategies for the sugarcane borer Diatraea saccharalis through the combined action of two parasitoid species: the egg parasitoid Trichogramma galloi and the larval parasitoid Cotesia flavipes. We describe the population dynamics with a six-dimensional host-parasitoid model in which host-parasitoid interactions are represented through a Holling Type II functional response, extending previous models by coupling egg and larval stage dynamics and incorporating parasitism saturation observed in laboratory experiments. We characterize the equilibrium structure of the model and analyze the local stability of the extinction equilibrium. Bifurcation analysis reveals that, for a wide range of Holling parameters, the pest population exceeds the economic damage threshold, motivating the design of active control strategies. We formulate and compare three biological control approaches: open-loop optimal control, State-Dependent Riccati Equation (SDRE) feedback control, and impulsive feedback control based on Lyapunov arguments. We perform numerical simulations to show that all three strategies successfully keep the pest population below the economic damage threshold. The impulsive strategy, in particular, achieves effective suppression with substantially fewer parasitoid releases than the continuous approaches, making it the most practically viable option for field implementation.

</details>


### [195] [Sharp Optimal Algorithm for Derivative-Free Stochastic Convex Optimization in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12938)
*Alexandra Carpentier,Chloé Rouyer,Alexandre Tsybakov,Arya Akhavan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究针对一维随机凸优化问题的零阶（仅函数值）反馈设定，提出了一种计算高效的算法，实现了与下界匹配的$O(1/\sqrt{T})$最优收敛速率，填补了该领域长期存在的对数差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一维随机凸优化问题中，零阶优化（仅通过带噪声的函数值进行优化）的已知上界与$\Omega(1/\sqrt{T})$下界之间存在对数级别的差距，这一差距长期未得到解决，亟需理论上的填补。

Method: 提出了一种计算高效的算法，专用于处理定义域$[0,1]$上、带有次高斯噪声的零阶oracle反馈的凸函数最小化问题。

Result: 该算法达到了$O(1/\sqrt{T})$的最优收敛速率，与已知下界相匹配，首次在该设定下提供了锐利的（sharp）速率保证。

Conclusion: 该工作成功闭合了一维随机凸优化零阶设定下的理论差距，证明了该设定下的最优收敛速率即为$O(1/\sqrt{T})$。

Abstract: Stochastic convex optimization is a classical problem with well-understood guarantees under first-order feedback. In contrast, for zero-order optimization with noisy function evaluations, a logarithmic gap has persisted between known upper bounds and the $Ω(1/\sqrt{T})$ lower bound, even in the one-dimensional case. In this work, we study the problem of minimizing a convex function $f : [0,1] \to [0,1]$ using a zero-order oracle with subGaussian noise. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm that achieves the optimal $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence rate, matching the lower bound. The result closes the existing gap in one dimension, providing the first sharp rate guarantee in this setting.

</details>


### [196] [Strict complementarity in semidefinite programming, singularity degree, and the (dis)connection of forward and backward errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12942)
*Gábor Pataki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了半定规划（SDP）中缺乏严格互补性时前向误差（真实误差）与后向误差（约束违反度）之间的巨大差距，提出了一个简单的规范形式来刻画这种缺乏严格互补性的SDP，并通过算法参数化所有此类SDP，最后通过计算实验发现前向-后向误差差距非常普遍，且两者呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格互补性在SDP的数值分析中至关重要，它驱动内点算法的收敛行为。当严格互补性失效时，前向误差（到最优解集的真实距离）与后向误差（约束违反度）之间存在显著差距，导致难以通过后向误差准确评估解的质量。因此需要系统刻画缺乏严格互补性的SDP结构并理解这种误差差距。

Method: (1) 提出一个简单的规范形式来刻画缺乏严格互补性的SDP，该规范形式通过初等行变换和旋转获得，使缺乏严格互补性显而易见，并能通过简单算法构造任何此类SDP；(2) 提出一种变体生成算法，可构造任何缺乏严格互补性但满足原始和对偶Slater条件的SDP，类似于Jordan标准型参数化矩阵的方式；(3) 精确刻画SDP的奇异度等于约束数量的条件；(4) 构建一组缺乏严格互补性的SDP并进行详细计算研究。

Result: (1) 成功提出了具有三个关键特征的规范形式；(2) 实现了对所有缺乏严格互补性的SDP数据的参数化；(3) 精确刻画了奇异度等于约束数的条件；(4) 计算实验发现：在许多小规模SDP（矩阵阶数≤20）中，前向误差超过后向误差高达七个数量级；在多个数据集中，前向误差与后向误差呈负相关——即真实误差越差，越难以通过后向误差检测到。

Conclusion: 缺乏严格互补性会导致SDP数值求解中前向误差与后向误差严重分离，且两者呈逆相关关系，使得基于约束违反度的后向误差无法可靠反映解的真实质量。所提出的规范形式和生成算法为理解和处理这类问题提供了系统性的理论框架和工具。

Abstract: Strict complementarity of a primal-dual pair of optimal solutions is fundamental in the numerical analysis of semidefinite programs (SDPs). Strict complementarity drives the convergence behavior of interior point algorithms. When it fails, pathological examples show a striking gap between two error measures of approximate solutions. The first of these is the forward or "true" error, i.e., the distance to the optimal solution set. The second is the less useful backward error, measured by the constraint violation.
  We first characterize the lack of strict complementarity in SDPs via a simple normal form. Our normal form has three key features: (i) it is obtained using elementary row operations and rotations; (ii) it makes the lack of strict complementarity evident; and (iii) it lets us construct any such SDP by a simple algorithm. A variant of our generating algorithm allows us to construct any SDP that fails strict complementarity but satisfies Slater's condition on both the primal and dual sides. Thus, we {\em parametrize} the data of all SDPs that lack strict complementarity in a manner similar to how the Jordan normal form parametrizes square matrices with given eigenvalue structure.
  Next, we precisely characterize when the singularity degree of an SDP equals the number of constraints -- a result that underlies our generating algorithms and that we believe is of independent interest.
  We construct and share a set of SDPs that lack strict complementarity and present a detailed computational study. We find that forward-backward error gaps are quite common: in many small SDPs (with matrix order $\leq 20$), the forward ("true") error exceeds the backward error by up to seven orders of magnitude. Further, in several data sets the forward and backward errors are {\em inversely} correlated. In other words, the worse the "true" forward error is, the harder it is to detect.

</details>


### [197] [Online Control via Counterfactual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13029)
*Yunzong Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“反事实追踪”的在线控制方法，能够在已知线性动力系统中与任意可模拟的因果策略类（包括非线性、动态策略）进行竞争，并建立了PAC-Bayes遗憾界。特别地，针对系统级响应球中的线性动态控制器类，首次实现了uniform regret guarantee，且匹配了信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线控制算法大多局限于线性控制器类，缺乏与更一般因果策略类（如非线性、动态策略）竞争的能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立统一且最优的遗憾保证。

Method: 方法名为反事实追踪（counterfactual tracking）：在每一轮，利用历史数据模拟基准策略的反事实状态-输入对，形成移动参考轨迹，然后使用一个固定的镇定控制器在物理系统上追踪该参考轨迹。该方法适用于任何可模拟的、每轮反事实状态-输入对直径有界的因果策略类。

Result: 建立了PAC-Bayes遗憾界，对任意后验分布成立，依赖其与先验的相对熵。对于有限类N个策略，当log N = O(T)时达到minimax最优的√(T log N)遗憾。对于系统级响应球中的线性动态控制器类，首次实现了uniform guarantee，并给出了匹配的常数级下界。

Conclusion: 该方法首次将在线控制的竞争范围扩展到一般因果策略类，并在线性动态控制器球类上取得了紧的遗憾界，为更广泛的在线控制场景提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We develop a method for online control that competes with general classes of causal policies, beyond the linear-controller classes used by most existing algorithms. Over a horizon of \(T\) rounds, we consider a known linear dynamical system subject to adversarial disturbances and convex costs revealed after each action. The method simulates the benchmark policies on the revealed history, uses their counterfactual state--input pairs to form a moving reference, and applies a fixed stabilizing controller to track that reference on the physical system. We call this method \emph{counterfactual tracking}.
  Counterfactual tracking applies to any measurable class of causal policies that can be simulated from the revealed history and whose counterfactual state--input pairs have bounded diameter at every round. The policies may be nonlinear or dynamic and need not share a parameterization. We establish PAC-Bayes regret guarantees that hold for every posterior over policies and depend on its relative entropy to a chosen prior. On a fixed plant with a tracker of bounded impulse-response gain, a finite class of \(N\) policies admits the minimax-optimal \(\sqrt{T\log N}\) dependence on \(T\) and \(N\) when \(\log N=O(T)\).
  As a central application, we compete with a system-level response ball of stabilizing linear dynamical controllers. The ball bounds the summed impulse-response deviation from the fixed tracker, but imposes no common decay envelope, memory length, or controller-order bound. To our knowledge, this is the first online-control guarantee uniform over such a class. A matching lower bound shows that our guarantee is tight up to constants.

</details>
